In Avalonia, add an All bindings browser with package/interface tree, search, version/source filters and separate columns for Mapped, Codec unsupported and Schema unknown, plus a separate runtime-availability column. Show declared Android requirements, observed backend/OS access and client MCP permission in three separate fields; an Android annotation never grants a client access. Bulk permission edits preview the exact versioned member set and do not silently grant future imports. The complete inventory remains visible even when invocation is denied.

Stage imports, validate recursive type limits and duplicate IDs, generate/verify dispatch artifacts, then atomically publish a new registry revision and invalidate affected approvals. A failed generator or Android marshalling gate leaves entries visible as unsupported; it must not fall back to arbitrary unvalidated parcels. Do not execute imported source code, deserialize arbitrary runtime types or silently widen a previously granted schema.


## Acceptance evidence

Compare every source member with the published registry; round-trip overloads and parcelables; test callbacks, handles, files and one-way methods; reject malformed recursive schemas and cross-client handles. Verify generated versus generic entry points share policy, and raw access never masquerades as method-isolated permission. Test annotation allOf/anyOf/conditional/read-write handling, absent metadata as unknown, SDK/OEM version drift, missing AIDL, non-SDK denial and pagination across imports. Add real Android instrumentation after mock marshalling and policy tests.


# Catalog compatibility and administration API

Treat the runtime catalog as a versioned data product. A companion SQLite DDL, JSON Schema and OpenAPI 3.1 contract define the machine interface. Keep catalog format version, source snapshot revision, entry revision, API version and published registry revision distinct. Stable entry IDs identify namespace plus logical qualified signature; schema/content hashes detect changed contracts. Never recycle deleted IDs or reinterpret an old schema version silently.


## Forward compatible catalog records

Use an extensible permission expression tree: permission, allOf, anyOf, conditional, read/write, manual enforcement, declared none and unknown, each with provenance and applicability. Preserve unrecognized extension nodes for round-trip storage, but report them unsupported for evaluation; unknown is never equivalent to permission-free. Readers reject unsupported major formats and negotiate documented additive minor changes. Migrations retain immutable source history.

| Storage record | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| catalog_snapshots | Immutable imported source set, source revisions/hashes, format version, coverage manifest and import status. Google-imported facts are never overwritten in place. |
| catalog_overlays | Stable entry ID, base snapshot/hash, overlay revision, local additions or patches, tombstone flag and provenance. Effective entries combine the selected immutable base with validated owner changes. |
| catalog_change_events | Actor, request ID, before/after revision, change kind, timestamp and publish result. Minimal audit; never store credentials or device-call payloads here. |




## Separate catalog CRUD API

`catalog/contracts/openapi.json` and `catalog/API-CONTRACT.md` are authoritative. There is one route family; no PATCH alias.

| API shape | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| GET /v1/catalog/entries | Search pinned registry/overlay view; expired cursor 410. |
| GET /v1/catalog/entries/{entry_id} | Effective entry and current cat1 ETag; conflicts visible, tombstone 410 with current ETag. |
| POST /v1/catalog/entries | Create local entry; canonical ID collision 409; Idempotency-Key required. |
| PUT /v1/catalog/entries/{entry_id} | If-Match current cat1 ETag, including conflicts/tombstones. Overlay mutation, restore or explicit rebase; no imported mutation. |
| DELETE /v1/catalog/entries/{entry_id} | If-Match; suppression/deletion tombstone; conflict requires explicit resolution first (409). |
| GET /v1/catalog/entries/{entry_id}/history | Immutable history and current administrative ETag for CAS. |
| POST /v1/catalog/imports | Idempotent immutable staging and validation, no active view change. |
| POST /v1/catalog/registries/{registry_id}/publish | Idempotent atomic publication of validated registry, overlay rebase/conflicts, generation increment and approval invalidation. |

Executable catalog DDL, configuration DDL, offline dispatch manifest generator and contract fixtures accompany this design. They specify and validate contracts; Android hosting and dispatch remain release gates requiring actual implementation/runtime evidence.

Catalog administration uses a distinct authenticated admin scope and explicit owner authorization, never an ordinary device-call permission. Reuse authenticated loopback transport protections or a separately approved management transport; localhost alone is not trusted. CRUD does not grant invocation rights and exposes no client credential or permission mutation as a normal agent tool. No administration endpoint is enabled by this design document.

Test source refresh with overlays, tombstone persistence, referenced-entry deletion, concurrent ETag edits, rollback, import/export round trips, unknown extension nodes, major-version rejection and no privilege gain after CRUD. Publishing altered schemas invalidates affected approvals and schema-bound grants until reevaluated.


# 19 Acceptance tests and delivery sequence

Build mock first, then prove the Android boundary. Passing policy tests alone does not establish compatibility, permissions, lifecycle reliability or store eligibility. Every gate produces retained test evidence and a pinned dependency manifest; there is no implementation claim in this proposal.

| Test area | Acceptance evidence |
| --- | --- |
| Policy properties | Unknown client/tool/schema, empty scopes and unknown constraint keys deny. Global deny dominates. Ask never widens scope. Output fields and pagination remain constrained. |
| Approval binding | Changing any argument, target, output field, credential epoch, policy revision or schema invalidates approval. Concurrent double use admits once. Expiry survives clock rollback; restart invalidates all approvals. |
| Authorization races | Barrier tests race Apply/Revoke with admission and SSE emission. No request admitted after the revocation commit uses old authority. In flight outcomes are labeled accurately. |
| Identity and transport | Spoofed clientInfo or package header cannot choose another principal. Missing auth, cross client session IDs, hostile Origin/Host, reconnect and oversized JSON are rejected. |
| Endpoint identity | A fake listener on the old port cannot obtain a credential in the preferred paired TLS profile. Compatibility clients and any lower assurance mode are explicitly documented. |
| SQLite durability | Constraint failures roll back all rule changes. Conflict edits never overwrite silently. Crash injection covers migration, policy commit, admission and outbox delivery; no permissive fallback. |
| Android integration | Release build invokes actual public API and a test AIDL service. Test runtime permission denial/revocation, WRITE_SETTINGS loss, brightness mode races, sensor cleanup, service death, signer/version change and Keystore invalidation. |
| Lifecycle | Test rotation, activity recreation, backgrounding, process kill, force stop, reboot, device lock and approved foreground service mode across supported Android versions and vendor devices. |
| UI accessibility | Phone and tablet, large text, screen reader and keyboard navigation work. Deny/Ask/Allow remains distinct from OS status. Dirty drafts, conflicts, revocation and expiry are understandable. |
| Privacy and performance | Automated secret canaries never appear in logs/exports. Limits stop queue growth. Measure request overhead, DB contention, memory and battery before setting service level targets. |




## Delivery sequence and exit criteria

1. Compatibility spike: prove Avalonia 12 Android release build, persistent/boot service, HTTP host, MCP baseline, SQLite, Keystore and full-source registry generation. Exit with supported device/client matrix and exact pinned versions.

1. Security core: implement policy shared by generated/generic/raw paths, marshalling, handle ownership, approvals, transactional edits and fake adapters. Exit with deterministic race and property tests passing.

1. Vertical slice: pair one MCP client and a self-ADB profile, read screen.state and issue one authorized input.tap against a test app. Verify image coordinates, stale-reference rejection, direct mode, unknown outcomes and configured Allow versus Ask.

1. Complete generic capabilities: validate settings/files/sensors and optional partner AIDL, per-client logging, external-host forwarding and any enabled Avalonia runtime adapter. Exit after restore, lifecycle and security gates; app-specific workflows remain outside the bridge.


# 20 Decisions and source references


## Decisions to settle before implementation

| Decision | Recommended starting point |
| --- | --- |
| Exposed surface | Complete declared SDK/AIDL/protocol inventory with generated and generic invocation; screen.state/input.tap and native wrappers are conveniences, not a curated limit. |
| Client authentication | Manual paired bearer profile for compatible on device agents; preferred pinned loopback TLS. Add standards based authorization only when required by target clients. |
| Runtime availability | Persistent foreground MCP service independent of UI, optional boot start, truthful AppBar/status notification, restart recovery and explicit OS-blocked states. |
| Android support floor | Set after compatibility spike and intended device inventory. Test real supported devices; do not infer support from successful desktop tests. |
| Privacy level | Local minimal audit and disabled client restore. Decide whether sensitive scope identifiers require encrypted storage and exports. |
| Partner Binder integration | Native Binder needs a known contract. Arbitrary-app UI control uses authorized ADB; optional Avalonia.RemoteControl requires an instrumented target and extra integration gates. |

## Architecture diagram

![Architecture diagram rendered with Pele](assets/architecture.svg)

Editable source follows.

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    Client[Agent or ADB-forwarded host] --> Server[Persistent MCP service]
    Server --> Gate[Shared client policy gate]
    Sources[SDK AIDL and protocol metadata] --> Registry[Full binding registry]
    Registry --> Gate
    Owner[Local owner UI] --> Gate
    Gate --> Binding[Generated or generic binding]
    Gate --> ADB[Paired ADB screen and tap]
    Gate --> Runtime[Optional Avalonia runtime]
    Gate --> Data[SQLite audit and configuration]
    Data --> Logs[Per-client optional logs]
```


## Request and approval sequence

![Request and approval sequence rendered with Pele](assets/request-sequence.svg)

Editable source follows.

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    participant C as Client
    participant P as Policy
    participant U as Owner
    participant G as Gate
    participant A as Adapter
    C->>P: Authenticated call
    P->>P: Validate plan
    opt Ask configured
        P->>U: Bound request
        U->>P: Decision
        break Denied or expired
            P-->>C: No dispatch
        end
    end
    P->>G: Plan and revisions
    G->>G: Recheck and audit
    G->>A: Typed operation
    A-->>P: Result or unknown
    P->>P: Filter and bounded log
    P-->>C: Response
    Note over G,A: No replay of uncertain taps
```


## Remediation contract precedence

This revision resolves recovered findings F-01–F-08 against current artifacts. Stable source-signature IDs are shared across imported occurrences and local overrides; registry schema/permission changes are revisions, not new random identities. No universal OEM/private introspection is promised: unsupported declarations and schemas remain discoverable with evidence and reasons. Catalog metadata is distinct from OS authority and MCP grants. `catalog/API-CONTRACT.md` defines all CRUD states, CAS and cursor rules; `catalog/bridge-config.sql` makes configuration invariants executable; `catalog/tooling/policy_reference.py` and `dispatch_manifest.py` provide offline contract fixtures, not a running bridge. Existing section diagrams remain editable Mermaid; Pele 1.10.0 rendering is a separate packaging check.

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/README.md ===
# Isolated contract remediation candidate

This directory is a design/schema/extractor candidate, not a new production catalog release. Historical release-002 facts below describe the separately owned source task. The current source task has retrieved SDK 37.2 source archive revision 1, verified its published SHA-1, and retained 16,910 files; it has not imported that Java archive into release-002. Source completeness remains incomplete. Current progress and exact hashes are frozen separately for each review. See API-CONTRACT.md for corrected authority, identity, state, ETag, CAS and cursor rules. No semantic namespace re-sharding is authorized or performed.

# Android binding and permission catalog — exact-version partial release

This is a populated, offline catalog of a pinned Android 17 release source snapshot. **It is not a complete catalog of the current SDK.** Google's repository metadata retrieved on 2026-10-26 lists non-preview SDK **37.2 revision 1** (extension 24). This catalog's public signature/annotation snapshot is **37.0**, from AOSP tag `android-17.0.0_r1` and `platform/prebuilts/sdk` commit `0f07e8d60ab0aba46129e427101f96ba459a95c1`. No equivalence to a downloaded SDK binary is claimed.

The primary runtime file is `output/release-002/catalog.sqlite`. `entries.jsonl` is the consolidated discovery export; `catalog.json` contains the same entry documents. Per-table JSONL preserves declaration variants, permission definitions, annotations and raw AIDL. `source-inventory.json` and `coverage-manifest.json` at the bundle root describe exactly which source paths were retrieved and which remain unavailable. All matching paths were enumerated in 102 declared repositories; individual APIs were not handpicked. Excluded repositories are listed in `project-coverage.json` and have unknown file coverage.

Use `bindings` for indexed package/owner/name lookup, `binding_declarations` for exact per-source evidence, `permission_relations` for permission ASTs, `permission_definitions` for manifest metadata, and `annotation_evidence` for external annotation XML. A public permission string constant in `permission_symbols` is **not** a complete manifest definition or proof that an OS build provides/grants it. Release-002 imported only 10 manifest definitions; the newer recovered core manifest contains 1,160 definitions and is not yet imported into that released database. `aidl_schemas` preserves complete acquired source text, imports and parse status. Method evidence retains directions, declared ordinal and explicit method IDs; **wire transaction codes, structured codecs, parcelable layouts, union tags and runtime availability remain unknown**.

`runtime_catalog` merges active local overlay documents with imported entries and excludes suppressed/deleted/conflict overlays. It includes source/test/frozen declarations; consumers must select the intended language, source surface and version using entry extensions and provenance. This is discovery metadata. It cannot authorize execution. OS authority and MCP client grants remain separate, unimplemented concerns. No Android bridge application, HTTP listener or CRUD service is included.

## Identity, versions and compatibility

Entry schema/API contract version remains `1.0.0`; corrected catalog storage baseline is `1.2.0`, SQLite `user_version=3`. The additive storage migration adds annotation/evidence tables, declaration hashes and an effective-entry projection. `schema.sql` is the concatenation of the two ordered migrations. This task corrects the isolated baseline migrations; originals are preserved under `../evidence`. Existing databases use checked offline copy migration `tooling/migrate_v3.py` to a new file; never rerun baseline migrations over a release.

Stable entry IDs are SHA-256 of a language-prefixed source signature: `java:owner#method(normalizedType,...)`, `java:owner#<init>(...)`, `java:owner#field`, or `aidl:descriptor#method(...)`. These are **source signature identities**, not validated JVM descriptors. Generic spelling changes may produce a different ID. A declaration revision is its canonical JSON SHA-256, scoped by immutable source ID and locator; changes in permission metadata do not overwrite earlier declarations. Current entry ETags use the single cat1 formula in API-CONTRACT.md, including current registry generation, base hash, identity, overlay revision and state. All source occurrences and their potentially different permissions remain available. Conflicting or source-dependent permission expressions produce an unknown aggregate rather than an invented union or intersection.

Unknown JSON semantics must fail closed for invocation; `x-*`/`extensions` retain additive metadata. The permission AST preserves `value`, `allOf`, `anyOf`, `conditional`, read/write scopes, explicit no-permission and manually enforced annotations. Unsupported annotation attributes and unresolved symbols remain unknown with raw text. Parameter annotations are retained separately and do not become callable-wide permission claims. AIDL interface annotations are retained as context without inventing compiler inheritance behavior. An absent annotation always remains unknown.

Imported tables are immutable. A future administration service uses local overlays and append-only history. `contracts/openapi.json` is an **OpenAPI 3.1 specification only**, covering entry CRUD, history, import and atomic registry publication. Its ETag/If-Match, conflict, idempotency and authorization rules are not an implemented HTTP service. Administrative catalog edits never grant Android or MCP permissions.

For updates, stage a new immutable catalog/registry, validate it, then publish its database by atomic rename or switch a same-filesystem pointer to the complete versioned directory. Readers pin one registry/view generation. Do not replace files inside an open release directory. A future overlay rebase compares base-record hashes; changed/deleted upstream entries become explicit conflicts requiring resolution. Tombstones remain auditable. Imported source IDs and provenance FKs may not be retargeted. `runtime_catalog` is a projection, not a substitute for these service transaction rules.

## Reproduce and validate

The extractor needs Python 3.10+ standard library. Validation/tests additionally use `jsonschema==4.26.0` (the tested environment was Python 3.14 / SQLite bundled with Python). From the bundle root:

```sh
python3 -m unittest discover -s catalog/tests -v
python3 catalog/tooling/build_catalog.py source-inventory.json /tmp/android-catalog-fresh-output
python3 catalog/tooling/validate_catalog.py /tmp/android-catalog-fresh-output source-inventory.json
```

The output directory must be empty. Input bytes must match both the pinned Git blob SHA-1 and SHA-256. Fixture tests ran before real parsing. Failed imports do not publish `catalog.sqlite`. Record/source ordering and hashes are deterministic for the same inventory; registry creation and validation timestamps vary. Source acquisition scripts and tree inventories are included for audit/reproduction. They must not be used to bypass a denial or silently retry unavailable sources. The original bulk downloader was stopped after sustained HTTP 429; a subsequent bounded first retrieval of the previously unreached SDK snapshot stopped on any error and succeeded. No denied path was retried. A future collection attempt requires fresh permitted access evidence and should honor rate limits.

See `output/release-002/validation-report.json` for executed schema/integrity/FK/count/reconciliation checks and exact limitations. Tests validate catalog behavior; they do not establish Android runtime behavior or Binder wire correctness. See the separate `hv-preliminary` receipt for the frozen hostile-validation scope; its initial snapshot predates the populated database.

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/API-CONTRACT.md ===
# Catalog administration contract 1.1

This specification and `contracts/openapi.json` describe an administration API; no listener, Android bridge or HTTP handler is implemented. The offline reference models and fixture databases test contract rules. The corrected storage baseline is 1.2.0 (`user_version=3`). Prior release migrations are evidence under `../evidence/`; do not run the corrected baseline on an existing database. `tooling/migrate_v3.py` performs a checked offline copy migration to a new file. Original files remain intact.

## Identity and authority

`entry_id = binding_id = lowercase SHA256(UTF8(canonical_signature))`. Canonical signatures are language-prefixed source signatures defined in README, with parser-normalized types and no source/version prefix. They are not erased JVM identities. One signature in two sources has one identity and two immutable occurrences. Permission changes create declaration revisions, not new IDs. A parser canonicalization change requires an explicit identity-version migration and reviewed grant invalidation; it cannot silently retarget grants. Local additions use exactly the same derivation. Imported ID/signature mismatch is a rejected import.

The three authorities are distinct: declared permission AST and provenance; OS runtime/backend capability; MCP client's exact binding/schema grants. CRUD changes metadata only. All generated/generic/known raw routes resolve to one canonical binding plan and policy evaluator; entry-point grants may restrict it. Known raw transaction requests must decode and validate under that schema or reject. Unknown raw requests require separate explicit broad backend/service authority and are visibly unable to enforce unknown method semantics; ordinary binding grants never imply it. Each alias carries exact schema hash, target and argument mapping hash, rechecked at admission. Unknown formats, codecs or required semantics remain visible and unavailable.

## Registry state and publication

SQL and JSON use `state` with staged, validated, rejected, published, retired. Create starts staged. Validation moves staged to validated or rejected. Rejected is terminal; correct bytes require a fresh immutable import ID. Publish accepts validated only, and atomically retires the old published registry, rebinds effective declarations, rebases overlays, records history/audit/outbox, increments registry generation, changes active pointer and invalidates affected approvals/schema grants. Only one published active registry is permitted by the service transaction. Re-publishing an already published registry under the same idempotency key returns the recorded result; a different key/current-active mismatch is 409. Retired cannot republish. Publication failure rolls back all changes, including generation. An import adds no active entries until publication. Validation never marks incomplete coverage complete.

Idempotency records are scoped by authenticated admin principal plus operation and key; retain request hash, response status/body and selected resource. Same canonical request hash replays original result; reuse with different hash is 409. Stage/import verifies hashes, source provenance, identity derivation, schema major/minor support, FK/count integrity and coverage declaration before validation. No network path or arbitrary filesystem path is accepted from a caller; local_artifact_id refers to a locally registered bounded artifact.

## Strong ETags, CAS and pagination

One strong ETag for all current entry representations is quoted `cat1:<registry_generation>:<imported_record_sha256-or-local>:<entry_id>:<overlay_revision>:<state>`. Base rows use overlay revision 0 and state active. Hash is the canonical effective imported document hash before local changes; local entries use literal `local`. Registry generation changes whenever the selected immutable base changes. Every overlay write MUST increase that entry's revision, including state-only transitions, and append history in the same transaction. Thus any effective representation change changes this ETag. Direct SQL overlay edits outside the administration transaction are unsupported. Canonical JSON is UTF-8, sorted object keys, compact separators, no NaN/Infinity; accepted schemas disallow ambiguous nonfinite values. No weak tags, wildcard or list preconditions.

GET by ID returns current ETag, including tombstone 410. Conflict GET is 200 with `state=conflict`, variants/base evidence and current ETag; it is never invocable. History GET is 200 with immutable history and **the current administrative ETag**, not a historical revision token. History entries' revisions are evidence only. Unknown ID is 404 without ETag. PUT/DELETE use that same current ETag even in conflict/tombstone states. Authentication/scope checks precede existence disclosure. Missing If-Match returns 428 before generic header validation; malformed precondition returns 400; stale tag returns 412 with current ETag, then transition-specific checks. This ordering is invariant across states.

Every overlay mutation increments global overlay_generation in its transaction. Search cursors pin `(registry_generation,overlay_generation,query_hash,last_entry_id)` and expire with 410 if either generation changed; mutable overlays are never paginated as if they were an old snapshot. Reissue the query from the start after expiry. Cursors are opaque authenticated bounded tokens, tied to the principal/query and expiry; tampered tokens are 400. Order is lexical entry_id. First and subsequent page reads pin the tuple in one read transaction. Search omits suppressed/deleted/conflict by default; administrative `include_suppressed=true` includes all three with state and ETag. Invocation discovery always omits them. A returned metadata page confers no grant.

## Entry transitions

| Current state | GET | POST same canonical ID | PUT with current tag | DELETE with current tag |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Missing | 404 | local active, revision 1 | 404 | 404 |
| Imported active, no overlay | 200 | 409 | active overlay revision 1 | suppressed overlay revision 1 |
| Local active | 200 | 409 | active revision +1 | deleted revision +1 |
| Imported override active | 200 | 409 | active revision +1 | suppressed revision +1 |
| Suppressed imported | 410 | 409 | restore active revision +1 | idempotent 200, unchanged revision/tag |
| Deleted local | 410 | 409 | restore active revision +1 | idempotent 200, unchanged revision/tag |
| Conflict | 200 | 409 | only explicit reviewed resolution against current base hash; otherwise 409 | 409 until resolved |

PUT never changes canonical ID or signature. `x-resolution` is required only for conflict: `{action: "rebase", expected_imported_record_sha256: "<current hash>"}`; null hash means the base is absent and explicit retain-local is selected with `action: "retain-local"`. A base-absent conflict resolves to a local active entry with the same ID; it must keep prior imported provenance in history, not a dangling imported FK. Invalid resolution or changed base returns 409; schema failures return 422. Restore requires a complete validated replacement Mutation, never an empty PATCH. A local addition followed by import of the same signature is always marked conflict during publication even if bytes happen to match. An import followed by local POST is 409. Neither path silently replaces or merges provenance. Upstream unchanged base preserves overlay; changed/missing base marks conflict, including suppression tombstones, so deletion intent and evidence remain auditable. No physical imported/history deletion occurs in CRUD.

Local override documents preserve reserved identity/provenance fields; API accepts only Mutation fields and constructs origin/state/registry_version/revisions itself. Sources and raw imported evidence are never editable via PUT. Imported provenance cannot be fabricated by local clients. Admin authentication uses a separate owner-authorized scope; ordinary MCP device grants cannot call this API. Concurrent compare/update happens inside one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction with an affected-row/CAS check and audit/history append before commit. Unknown schema major/required semantics returns 422. All failures leave state/generations unchanged.

## Deliverable boundary and coverage

The generator emits a searchable complete inventory of acquired declarations, including unsupported/unreachable entries and reasons. It does not assert universal discovery of private/OEM surfaces. AIDL ordinals and declared method IDs are evidence, never wire transaction codes without generated/compiler proof. Public binding/permission coverage is measured against source inventories by namespace/version/kind and unresolved records; absent annotation is unknown. Source task owns acquisition/current production imports; release-002's 135,508 records and earlier pre-import 0 records are different frozen states. Source completeness remains an overall acceptance blocker until an independent current review establishes otherwise. Namespace/package modules plus global indexes/permissions/types/evidence/overrides remain a proposal; no semantic re-sharding is performed here.

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/schema.sql ===
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
PRAGMA user_version=1;
CREATE TABLE catalog_meta(key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,value TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE sources(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, path TEXT NOT NULL, url TEXT NOT NULL, revision TEXT NOT NULL, retrieved_at TEXT NOT NULL, sha256 TEXT NOT NULL, api_level INTEGER NOT NULL, channel TEXT NOT NULL, kind TEXT NOT NULL, license TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE bindings(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, signature TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE, package TEXT NOT NULL, owner TEXT, kind TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, return_type TEXT, arguments_json TEXT, raw_signature TEXT NOT NULL, codec_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'unknown');
CREATE INDEX binding_lookup ON bindings(package,owner,name,binding_id);
CREATE TABLE occurrences(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES bindings, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, line INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,source_id,line));
CREATE TABLE binding_declarations(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL,source_id TEXT NOT NULL,line INTEGER NOT NULL,document_json TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,source_id,line),FOREIGN KEY(binding_id,source_id,line) REFERENCES occurrences);
CREATE TABLE permission_definitions(name TEXT NOT NULL, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, protection_level TEXT, attributes_json TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(name,source_id));
CREATE TABLE permission_relations(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES bindings,source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources,line INTEGER NOT NULL,annotation TEXT,logic TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(logic IN ('all','any','permission','access','conditional','manual','explicit-none','unknown')),expression_json TEXT NOT NULL,raw_annotation TEXT);
CREATE INDEX permission_lookup ON permission_relations(binding_id);
CREATE TABLE aidl_schemas(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, package TEXT, descriptor TEXT, declaration_kind TEXT, raw_schema TEXT NOT NULL, parse_status TEXT NOT NULL, transaction_metadata_json TEXT NOT NULL, codec_status TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE issues(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, source_id TEXT REFERENCES sources,line INTEGER,category TEXT NOT NULL,reason TEXT NOT NULL,raw TEXT);
-- Contract 1.0.0: source records are append-only. Imports run in one transaction.
CREATE TRIGGER sources_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported sources are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER sources_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported sources are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER bindings_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON bindings BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported bindings are immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER bindings_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON bindings BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Suppress imported records using a local overlay'); END;
CREATE TABLE registries(registry_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, registry_version INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE, schema_version TEXT NOT NULL, android_version TEXT NOT NULL, api_level INTEGER NOT NULL, source_set_hash TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('staged','validated','rejected','published','retired')), validation_json TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE registry_sources(registry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registries,source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources,PRIMARY KEY(registry_id,source_id));
CREATE TABLE active_registry(singleton INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton=1),registry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES registries);
CREATE TABLE overlays(entry_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, imported_binding_id TEXT REFERENCES bindings, canonical_signature TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(revision>0), base_registry_id TEXT REFERENCES registries, base_record_hash TEXT, state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('active','suppressed','conflict','deleted')), document_json TEXT NOT NULL, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX overlay_lookup ON overlays(imported_binding_id,state);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX overlay_one_import ON overlays(imported_binding_id) WHERE imported_binding_id IS NOT NULL;
CREATE TABLE entry_history(entry_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES overlays, revision INTEGER NOT NULL, operation TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(operation IN ('create','update','suppress','delete','restore','rebase','conflict')), document_json TEXT NOT NULL, actor TEXT NOT NULL, occurred_at TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(entry_id,revision));
CREATE TRIGGER history_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON entry_history BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'History is append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER history_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON entry_history BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'History is append-only'); END;
CREATE TABLE import_jobs(idempotency_key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,request_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL,registry_id TEXT REFERENCES registries,state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('staged','validated','rejected','published','retired')),result_json TEXT NOT NULL);
-- Bounded effective-view readers select current registry then join occurrences,
-- registry_sources and overlays. They MUST exclude suppressed/deleted/conflict
-- rows for invocation discovery and reject unknown semantic extensions.

CREATE TRIGGER occurrences_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON occurrences BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER occurrences_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON occurrences BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER binding_declarations_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON binding_declarations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER binding_declarations_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON binding_declarations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_definitions_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_definitions BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_definitions_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_definitions BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_relations_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_relations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER permission_relations_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_relations BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER aidl_schemas_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON aidl_schemas BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER aidl_schemas_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON aidl_schemas BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER issues_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON issues BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER issues_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON issues BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER registry_sources_immutable_update BEFORE UPDATE ON registry_sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

CREATE TRIGGER registry_sources_immutable_delete BEFORE DELETE ON registry_sources BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported source records are append-only'); END;

-- Additive migration from version 1. Entry/API JSON contracts retain version 1.0.0;
-- additions live under their explicitly reserved extensions/x-* namespaces.
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
CREATE TABLE source_details(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)));
CREATE TABLE parse_reports(source_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES sources, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)));
ALTER TABLE binding_declarations ADD COLUMN record_sha256 TEXT;
CREATE TABLE annotation_evidence(source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, locator TEXT NOT NULL, binding_id TEXT REFERENCES bindings, scope TEXT NOT NULL, document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), PRIMARY KEY(source_id,locator));
CREATE INDEX annotation_target ON annotation_evidence(binding_id);
CREATE TABLE permission_symbols(symbol TEXT NOT NULL, value TEXT NOT NULL, source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES sources, line INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(symbol,source_id,line));
CREATE TABLE effective_entries(entry_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES bindings(binding_id), document_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(document_json)), record_sha256 TEXT NOT NULL, etag TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TRIGGER source_details_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON source_details BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER source_details_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON source_details BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER annotation_evidence_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON annotation_evidence BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER annotation_evidence_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON annotation_evidence BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER permission_symbols_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON permission_symbols BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER permission_symbols_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON permission_symbols BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Imported evidence is immutable'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER effective_entries_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON effective_entries BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publish a new registry instead'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER effective_entries_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON effective_entries BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Publish a new registry instead'); END;
-- ETag composition is identical for base, overlay, tombstone and history CAS.
CREATE TABLE catalog_view(singleton INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton=1),registry_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(registry_generation>0),overlay_generation INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(overlay_generation>=0));
INSERT INTO catalog_view VALUES(1,1,0);
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_generation_insert AFTER INSERT ON overlays BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET overlay_generation=overlay_generation+1; END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_generation_update AFTER UPDATE ON overlays BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET overlay_generation=overlay_generation+1; END;
CREATE TRIGGER overlay_generation_delete AFTER DELETE ON overlays BEGIN UPDATE catalog_view SET overlay_generation=overlay_generation+1; END;
CREATE VIEW runtime_catalog AS
 SELECT e.entry_id,coalesce(o.document_json,e.document_json) AS document_json,
 '"cat1:'||v.registry_generation||':'||e.record_sha256||':'||e.entry_id||':'||coalesce(o.revision,0)||':'||coalesce(o.state,'active')||'"' AS etag
 FROM effective_entries e CROSS JOIN catalog_view v LEFT JOIN overlays o ON o.imported_binding_id=e.entry_id
 WHERE o.entry_id IS NULL OR o.state='active'
 UNION ALL
 SELECT o.entry_id,o.document_json,'"cat1:'||v.registry_generation||':local:'||o.entry_id||':'||o.revision||':'||o.state||'"'
 FROM overlays o CROSS JOIN catalog_view v WHERE o.imported_binding_id IS NULL AND o.state='active';
PRAGMA user_version=3;
COMMIT;

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/bridge-config.sql ===
-- Executable DESIGN fixture for the future bridge's app-private configuration DB.
-- No tokens, devices, service, installed settings or real client records are created.
PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON;
BEGIN IMMEDIATE;
CREATE TABLE clients(client_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,display_name TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('active','disabled','revoked')),credential_epoch INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(credential_epoch>=0),row_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(row_version>=1),created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,last_seen_at INTEGER);
CREATE TABLE credentials(credential_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients ON DELETE RESTRICT,verifier BLOB NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK(length(verifier)=32),key_alias TEXT NOT NULL,state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('active','revoked')),created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,expires_at INTEGER,revoked_at INTEGER,epoch INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(epoch>=0));
CREATE TABLE binding_definitions(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY CHECK(length(binding_id)=64),canonical_signature TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,entry_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE binding_mappings(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES binding_definitions,schema_hash TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(schema_hash)=64),registry_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(registry_revision>=1),mapping_state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(mapping_state IN ('known','unknown','unsupported','unreachable')),reason_code TEXT,dispatch_kind TEXT NOT NULL,descriptor TEXT,transaction_code INTEGER,argument_schema_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(argument_schema_json)),permission_ast_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(permission_ast_json)),PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,schema_hash));
CREATE TABLE binding_occurrences(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES binding_definitions,source_id TEXT NOT NULL,locator TEXT NOT NULL,record_hash TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(record_hash)=64),provenance_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(provenance_json)),PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,source_id,locator));
CREATE TABLE tools(tool_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,schema_version TEXT NOT NULL,adapter_version TEXT NOT NULL,capability_key TEXT NOT NULL,scope_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL,risk_class TEXT NOT NULL,registry_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(registry_revision>=1));
CREATE TABLE tool_binding_aliases(tool_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tools,binding_id TEXT NOT NULL,schema_hash TEXT NOT NULL,canonicalization_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(canonicalization_version>=1),argument_mapping_hash TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(argument_mapping_hash)=64),PRIMARY KEY(tool_id,binding_id),FOREIGN KEY(binding_id,schema_hash) REFERENCES binding_mappings);
CREATE TABLE client_binding_rules(client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients ON DELETE CASCADE,binding_id TEXT NOT NULL,schema_hash TEXT NOT NULL,effect TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(effect IN ('deny','ask','allow')),constraints_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(constraints_json)),row_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(row_version>=1),PRIMARY KEY(client_id,binding_id),FOREIGN KEY(binding_id,schema_hash) REFERENCES binding_mappings);
CREATE TABLE client_tool_rules(client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients ON DELETE CASCADE,tool_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tools,tool_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL,effect TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(effect IN ('deny','ask','allow')),scope_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(scope_json)),scope_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL,updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(client_id,tool_id));
CREATE TABLE tool_guardrails(tool_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES tools,disabled INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(disabled IN (0,1)),require_ask INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(require_ask IN (0,1)),scope_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(scope_json)),scope_schema_version TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE policy_state(singleton_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton_id=1),policy_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(policy_revision>=1),registry_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(registry_revision>=1),emergency_deny INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(emergency_deny IN (0,1)),updated_at INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE executions(execution_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients,credential_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES credentials,tool_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tools,request_digest BLOB NOT NULL CHECK(length(request_digest)=32),boot_id TEXT NOT NULL,policy_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(policy_revision>=1),state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('admitted','completed','failed','canceled','unknown')),admitted_at INTEGER NOT NULL,completed_at INTEGER,idempotency_key TEXT);
CREATE TABLE audit_events(event_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,occurred_at INTEGER NOT NULL,client_id TEXT REFERENCES clients,tool_id TEXT REFERENCES tools,operation TEXT NOT NULL,decision TEXT NOT NULL,reason_code TEXT NOT NULL,policy_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(policy_revision>=1),execution_id TEXT REFERENCES executions,duration_ms INTEGER CHECK(duration_ms>=0));
CREATE TABLE change_outbox(change_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,policy_revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(policy_revision>=1),change_kind TEXT NOT NULL,affected_client_id TEXT REFERENCES clients,created_at INTEGER NOT NULL,delivered_at INTEGER);
CREATE TABLE schema_migrations(version INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(version>=1),checksum TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(length(checksum)=64),applied_at INTEGER NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE connection_profiles(connection_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(mode IN ('self-adb','host-adb','remote-control')),display_name TEXT NOT NULL,enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(enabled IN (0,1)),endpoint_reference TEXT NOT NULL,pairing_key_alias TEXT,revision INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(revision>=1));
CREATE TABLE targets(target_key TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,connection_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES connection_profiles,display_name TEXT NOT NULL,build_identity TEXT,configuration_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(configuration_json)));
CREATE TABLE binding_availability(binding_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES binding_definitions,target_key TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES targets,state TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(state IN ('available','unavailable','unknown')),reason_code TEXT NOT NULL,os_build TEXT NOT NULL,checked_at INTEGER NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(binding_id,target_key));
CREATE TABLE service_preferences(singleton_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(singleton_id=1),boot_start INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(boot_start IN (0,1)),desired_running INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(desired_running IN (0,1)),manual_stop INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK(manual_stop IN (0,1)));
CREATE TABLE client_logging_policy(client_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES clients,mode TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(mode IN ('off','metadata','diagnostic')),retention_days INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(retention_days BETWEEN 0 AND 365),max_bytes INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(max_bytes>=0),redaction_schema_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(redaction_schema_version>=1),row_version INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(row_version>=1));
CREATE TABLE call_logs(log_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,client_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clients,execution_id TEXT REFERENCES executions,occurred_at INTEGER NOT NULL,projection_json TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(json_valid(projection_json)),expires_at INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(expires_at>=occurred_at));
CREATE INDEX call_logs_expiry ON call_logs(expires_at);
CREATE INDEX audit_client_time ON audit_events(client_id,occurred_at);
CREATE TRIGGER audit_no_update BEFORE UPDATE ON audit_events BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Audit is append-only'); END;
CREATE TRIGGER audit_no_delete BEFORE DELETE ON audit_events BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Audit retention uses a separately authorized maintenance transaction'); END;
COMMIT;

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/build_catalog.py ===
"""Offline reproducible catalog import. No network, devices, or bridge application.
Usage: python build_catalog.py INVENTORY OUTPUT
Sources' local_path is relative to inventory.parent. Refuses populated output.
"""
import pathlib,json,sqlite3,hashlib,datetime,sys,xml.etree.ElementTree as ET,zipfile,collections,os,re
from parsers import signature,aidl,permission,unknown,sha,split_top,typ
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
def js(x):return json.dumps(x,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False)
def hb(b):return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()
def ref(text):return dict(text=text,status='unknown',codec_status='unknown',reason='Source type retained; runtime resolver/codec not verified')
def _build(inventory,output):
 invpath=pathlib.Path(inventory).resolve();root=invpath.parent;spec=json.loads(invpath.read_text());out=pathlib.Path(output).resolve()
 out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
 if list(out.iterdir()):raise ValueError('Output must be empty; preserve immutable earlier publication')
 sources=[s for s in spec['sources'] if s['status']=='downloaded']
 db=sqlite3.connect(out/'catalog.staging.sqlite');db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON');db.execute('PRAGMA cache_size=-131072');db.execute('PRAGMA temp_store=MEMORY')
 db.executescript((P/'migrations/001-initial.sql').read_text());db.executescript((P/'migrations/002-evidence-and-publication.sql').read_text())
 db.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
 for s in sources:
  b=(root/s['local_path']).read_bytes()
  if hb(b)!=s['sha256']:raise ValueError('SHA256 mismatch '+s['path'])
  if hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(b)).encode()+b'\0'+b).hexdigest()!=s['git_blob']:raise ValueError('Git blob mismatch '+s['path'])
  s['source_id']=sha(s['url']+'@'+s['revision']+':'+s['sha256'])
  db.execute('INSERT INTO sources VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(s['source_id'],s['path'],s['url'],s['revision'],s['retrieved_at'],s['sha256'],37,'stable',s['kind'],s['license']))
  db.execute('INSERT INTO source_details VALUES (?,?)',(s['source_id'],js(s)))
 source_by_id={s['source_id']:s for s in sources};symbols_by_value=collections.defaultdict(set);symbol_rows=[]
 # Separate constant pass avoids source order changing symbolic permission resolution.
 for s in sources:
  if s['kind'] not in ('sdk-signature','source-signature'):continue
  for r in signature((root/s['local_path']).read_text()):
   if r['kind']=='field' and r['extras'].get('constant_raw'):
    value=r['extras']['constant_raw']
    try:value=json.loads(value)
    except ValueError:continue
    if isinstance(value,str) and ('.permission.' in value or (r['owner'] or '').endswith('.permission')):
     symbol=r['owner']+'.'+r['name'];symbols_by_value[symbol].add(value);symbol_rows.append((symbol,value,s['source_id'],r['line']))
 symbols={k:next(iter(v)) for k,v in symbols_by_value.items() if len(v)==1}
 db.executemany('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO permission_symbols VALUES (?,?,?,?)',symbol_rows)
 def issue(s,line,reason,raw,category='unsupported'):
  db.execute('INSERT INTO issues(source_id,line,category,reason,raw) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',(s['source_id'],line,category,reason,raw))
 def add(r,s,lang='java'):
  canonical=lang+':'+r['canonical'];bid=sha(canonical);line=r['line'];ast=permission(r['annotations'],symbols,lang=='aidl')
  doc=dict(schema_version='1.0.0',entry_id=bid,canonical_signature=canonical,kind=r['kind'],name=r['name'],package=r['package'],permission=ast,provenance=[dict(source_id=s['source_id'],line=line,raw_signature=r['raw'],source_revision=s['revision'])],origin='imported',state='active',registry_version=1,overlay_revision=0,codec_status='unknown',unresolved=['OS authority, MCP client grants and wire codecs are not established by catalog metadata'],required_semantics=[],extensions=dict(language=lang,source_kind=s['kind'],source_path=s['path'],source_scope=s['scope'],annotations=r['all_annotations'],parameters=r['parameters'],**r['extras']))
  if r['owner'] is not None:doc['owner']=r['owner']
  for parameter in doc['extensions']['parameters']:parameter['permission']=permission(parameter['annotations'],symbols,lang=='aidl')
  doc['arguments']=[ref(x['type']) for x in r['parameters']]
  if r['return_type'] is not None:doc['return_type']=ref(r['return_type'])
  if r['kind']=='unsupported':doc['unresolved'].append(r['extras'].get('parse_error','Structured AIDL members unresolved'))
  db.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO bindings(binding_id,signature,package,owner,kind,name,return_type,arguments_json,raw_signature) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,canonical,r['package'],r['owner'],r['kind'],r['name'],r['return_type'],js([x['type'] for x in r['parameters']]),r['raw']))
  # Duplicate declaration at the exact same locator is a defect, never silently ignored.
  db.execute('INSERT INTO occurrences VALUES (?,?,?)',(bid,s['source_id'],line))
  db.execute('INSERT INTO binding_declarations VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,s['source_id'],line,js(doc),sha(js(doc))))
  db.execute('INSERT INTO permission_relations(binding_id,source_id,line,annotation,logic,expression_json,raw_annotation) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,s['source_id'],line,','.join(a['name'] for a in r['annotations']) or None,ast['op'],js(ast),js(r['annotations'])))
  if r['kind']=='unsupported':issue(s,line,r['extras'].get('parse_error','Structured AIDL body retained without semantic parse'),r['raw'])
  return bid
 totals=collections.Counter();per_source=[]
 for i,s in enumerate(sources):
  kind=s['kind'];sid=s['source_id'];path=root/s['local_path'];report=dict(source_id=sid,kind=kind,input_bytes=s['size_bytes'],declarations=0,unsupported=0,status='retained')
  if kind in ('sdk-signature','source-signature'):
   text=path.read_text();expected=sum(bool(l.strip()) and not l.strip().startswith('//') and l.strip()!='}' for l in text.splitlines())
   for r in signature(text):add(r,s);report['declarations']+=1;report['unsupported']+=r['kind']=='unsupported'
   if expected!=report['declarations']:raise ValueError('Signature reconciliation failed '+s['path'])
   report.update(expected_declarations=expected,status='partial' if report['unsupported'] else 'parsed')
  elif kind=='manifest':
   try:
    xml=ET.fromstring(path.read_bytes());ns='{http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android}'
    defs=list(xml.iter('permission'));report['permission_elements']=len(defs)
    for node in defs:
     name=node.attrib.get(ns+'name')
     if name is None:issue(s,None,'Permission element lacks name',ET.tostring(node,encoding='unicode'));continue
     try:db.execute('INSERT INTO permission_definitions VALUES (?,?,?,?)',(name,sid,node.attrib.get(ns+'protectionLevel'),js(dict(attributes=node.attrib,children=[ET.tostring(c,encoding='unicode') for c in node]))))
     except sqlite3.IntegrityError:issue(s,None,'Duplicate permission name in one manifest; raw element retained',ET.tostring(node,encoding='unicode'),'duplicate')
    report['status']='parsed'
   except ET.ParseError as e:issue(s,None,'Manifest XML unresolved: '+str(e),path.read_text(errors='replace'));report['status']='unsupported'
  elif kind=='aidl':
   text=path.read_text();d=aidl(text)
   db.execute('INSERT INTO aidl_schemas VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(sid,d['package'],d['descriptor'] if d['declaration_kind']=='interface' else None,d['declaration_kind'],text,d['parse_status'],js(dict(imports=d['imports'],declaration_annotations=d['annotations'],descriptor_candidate=d['descriptor'],oneway=d.get('oneway'),wire_status='unknown')), 'unknown'))
   if d['descriptor']:
    r=dict(kind='aidl-interface' if d['declaration_kind']=='interface' else 'unsupported',canonical=d['descriptor'],package=d['package'],owner=d['descriptor'],name=d['descriptor'].split('.')[-1],return_type=None,parameters=[],annotations=d['annotations'],all_annotations=d['annotations'],raw=text.split('{',1)[0].strip(),line=1,extras=dict(aidl_kind=d['declaration_kind'],imports=d['imports'],parse_status=d['parse_status'],oneway=d.get('oneway')))
    add(r,s,'aidl');report['declarations']+=1;report['unsupported']+=r['kind']=='unsupported'
   for r in d['members']:
    r['extras']['interface_annotations']=d['annotations'];r['extras']['interface_permission']=permission(d['annotations'],symbols,True)
    add(r,s,'aidl');report['declarations']+=1;report['unsupported']+=r['kind']=='unsupported'
   report.update(status=d['parse_status'],member_chunks=len(d['members']))
   if d['parse_status']!='methods-parsed':issue(s,None,d['reason'],text,'aidl-semantic-gap')
  elif kind=='sdk-annotations':report['status']='deferred-external-annotation-pass'
  elif kind in ('evidence','sdk-history'):report['status']='retained-evidence-not-current-declarations'
  else:issue(s,None,'Unrecognized source kind',kind);report['status']='unsupported'
  per_source.append(report);totals[kind]+=1
  if i%200==0:print('parsed sources',i,'/',len(sources),flush=True)
 # External annotations are retained in full and mapped only when an exact source signature proves the target.
 for s in sources:
  if s['kind']!='sdk-annotations':continue
  report=next(x for x in per_source if x['source_id']==s['source_id']);report.update(xml_documents=0,items=0,annotations=0,mapped_annotations=0,status='parsed')
  with zipfile.ZipFile(root/s['local_path']) as archive:
   for filename in sorted(archive.namelist()):
    if not filename.endswith('.xml'):issue(s,None,'Unrecognized annotation archive member',filename);continue
    data=ET.fromstring(archive.read(filename));report['xml_documents']+=1
    for ordinal,item in enumerate(data.findall('item')):
     report['items']+=1;name=item.attrib.get('name','');scope='declaration';target=name
     pidx=re.search(r'\s+(\d+)$',target)
     if pidx:scope='parameter:'+pidx.group(1);target=target[:pidx.start()]
     owner,sep,tail=target.partition(' ')
     canonical=None
     if not sep:canonical=owner
     elif '(' in tail:
      a=tail.find('(');b=tail.rfind(')');m=re.search(r'([\w$]+)$',tail[:a]);method=m.group(1) if m else None
      if method:canonical=owner+'#'+('<init>' if method==owner.split('.')[-1] else method)+'('+','.join(typ(x) for x in split_top(tail[a+1:b]) if x.strip())+')'
     else:canonical=owner+'#'+tail.split()[-1]
     bid=sha('java:'+canonical) if canonical else None
     if bid and not db.execute('SELECT 1 FROM bindings WHERE binding_id=?',(bid,)).fetchone():bid=None
     anns=[]
     for ai,node in enumerate(item.findall('annotation')):
      a=dict(name=node.attrib.get('name',''),arguments=','.join(v.attrib.get('name','')+'='+v.attrib.get('val','') for v in node.findall('val')),raw=ET.tostring(node,encoding='unicode'),values=[dict(v.attrib) for v in node.findall('val')]);anns.append(a)
      a['permission']=permission([a],symbols)
      evidence=dict(item_name=name,archive_member=filename,item_ordinal=ordinal,annotation=a,target_resolution='exact' if bid else 'unknown')
      db.execute('INSERT INTO annotation_evidence VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',(s['source_id'],filename+':'+str(ordinal)+':'+str(ai),bid,scope,js(evidence)));report['annotations']+=1;report['mapped_annotations']+=bid is not None
     if bid and scope=='declaration':
      ast=permission(anns,symbols)
      if ast['op']!='unknown':db.execute('INSERT INTO permission_relations(binding_id,source_id,line,annotation,logic,expression_json,raw_annotation) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,s['source_id'],ordinal+1,'external',ast['op'],js(ast),js(anns)))
 for report in per_source:db.execute('INSERT INTO parse_reports VALUES (?,?)',(report['source_id'],js(report)))
 # One conservative discovery entry; all occurrence documents remain authoritative evidence.
 for (bid,) in db.execute('SELECT binding_id FROM bindings ORDER BY binding_id').fetchall():
  rows=db.execute('SELECT source_id,line,document_json FROM binding_declarations WHERE binding_id=? ORDER BY source_id,line',(bid,)).fetchall()
  def rank(row):
   s=source_by_id[row[0]]
   return (0 if s['kind']=='sdk-signature' else 1 if s['kind']=='source-signature' and s['path'].split('/')[-1]=='current.txt' else 2 if s['kind']=='source-signature' else 3,s['path'])
  rows.sort(key=rank);doc=json.loads(rows[0][2]);doc['provenance']=[p for row in rows for p in json.loads(row[2])['provenance']]
  expressions=[json.loads(row[0]) for row in db.execute('SELECT expression_json FROM permission_relations WHERE binding_id=?',(bid,))]
  def semantic(a):
   return {k:semantic(v) for k,v in a.items() if not k.startswith('x-')} if isinstance(a,dict) else [semantic(v) for v in a] if isinstance(a,list) else a
  known={js(semantic(a)):a for a in expressions if not (a['op']=='unknown' and a.get('reason','').startswith('No declared recognized'))}
  if len(known)==1:doc['permission']=next(iter(known.values()))
  elif len(known)>1:doc['permission']=unknown('Source-dependent or conflicting permission metadata; select exact declaration evidence');doc['extensions']['permission_variants']=list(known.values())
  doc['extensions']['occurrence_count']=len(rows);content=js(doc);digest=sha(content)
  db.execute('INSERT INTO effective_entries VALUES (?,?,?,?)',(bid,content,digest,'"cat1:1:'+digest+':'+bid+':0:active"'))
 set_hash=sha(js(sorted(s['source_id'] for s in sources)));registry_id=sha('registry:'+set_hash)
 counts={name:db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM '+name).fetchone()[0] for name in ('sources','bindings','occurrences','binding_declarations','permission_definitions','permission_symbols','permission_relations','aidl_schemas','annotation_evidence','issues','effective_entries')}
 validation=dict(integrity_check=db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0],foreign_key_check=db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall(),counts=counts,schema_user_version=db.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0],source_reports=per_source,unavailable_paths=sum(s['status']!='downloaded' for s in spec['sources']),complete=False)
 if validation['integrity_check']!='ok' or validation['foreign_key_check']:raise ValueError('Integrity/FK validation failed')
 if counts['bindings']!=counts['effective_entries'] or counts['occurrences']!=counts['binding_declarations']:raise ValueError('Record reconciliation failed')
 db.execute('INSERT INTO registries VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(registry_id,1,'1.2.0','android-17.0.0_r1',37,set_hash,datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),'published',js(validation)))
 db.executemany('INSERT INTO registry_sources VALUES (?,?)',[(registry_id,s['source_id']) for s in sources]);db.execute('INSERT INTO active_registry VALUES (1,?)',(registry_id,))
 db.executemany('INSERT INTO catalog_meta VALUES (?,?)',[('schema_version','1.2.0'),('entry_contract_version','1.0.0'),('source_set_hash',set_hash),('coverage','PARTIAL: SDK37.0 complete declared snapshot; current SDK37.2 not acquired; AIDL/manifests bounded partial source corpus'),('source_inventory_sha256',hb(invpath.read_bytes())),('authority','Metadata only; no OS capability or MCP client grant'),('sdk_api_level','37.0'),('current_sdk_metadata_api_level','37.2')])
 db.commit();db.execute('PRAGMA optimize');db.close();os.replace(out/'catalog.staging.sqlite',out/'catalog.sqlite')
 db=sqlite3.connect(out/'catalog.sqlite');db.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
 for table in ('sources','source_details','bindings','occurrences','binding_declarations','permission_definitions','permission_symbols','permission_relations','aidl_schemas','annotation_evidence','issues','parse_reports'):
  with (out/(table+'.jsonl')).open('w') as f:
   for row in db.execute('SELECT * FROM '+table):f.write(js(dict(row))+'\n')
 with (out/'entries.jsonl').open('w') as f:
  for row in db.execute('SELECT document_json FROM effective_entries ORDER BY entry_id'):f.write(row[0]+'\n')
 # JSON export is streamed to avoid loading the catalog into memory.
 with (out/'catalog.json').open('w') as f:
  f.write('{"schema_version":"1.2.0","complete":false,"entries":[');first=True
  for row in db.execute('SELECT document_json FROM effective_entries ORDER BY entry_id'):
   if not first:f.write(',')
   f.write(row[0]);first=False
  f.write(']}')
 db.close();(out/'validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(validation,indent=2));print(json.dumps(dict(counts=counts,integrity_check=validation['integrity_check'],unavailable_paths=validation['unavailable_paths']),indent=2),flush=True)
 return validation
def build(inventory,output):
 out=pathlib.Path(output).resolve()
 if out.exists() and list(out.iterdir()):raise ValueError('Output must be empty; preserve immutable earlier publication')
 try:return _build(inventory,output)
 except Exception as error:
  out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  (out/'validation-failure.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(published=False,error_type=type(error).__name__,error=str(error)),indent=2))
  raise
if __name__=='__main__':build(sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2])

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/contract_reference.py ===
"""Small offline state-machine oracle for API fixtures, NOT an administration service."""
import hashlib,json

class Error(Exception):
    def __init__(self,status):self.status=status

def identity(signature):return hashlib.sha256(signature.encode()).hexdigest()
def digest(document):return hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(document,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False,ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()

class Catalog:
    def __init__(self):self.entries={};self.registry_generation=1;self.overlay_generation=0;self.history={}
    def tag(self,key):
        r=self.entries[key]
        return f'"cat1:{self.registry_generation}:{r["base_hash"] or "local"}:{key}:{r["revision"]}:{r["state"]}"'
    def create(self,signature,document):
        key=identity(signature)
        if key in self.entries:raise Error(409)
        self.entries[key]=dict(signature=signature,document=document,state='active',revision=1,base_hash=None,imported=False)
        self.record(key,'create');return key
    def record(self,key,operation):
        self.overlay_generation+=1
        self.history.setdefault(key,[]).append(dict(operation=operation,revision=self.entries[key]['revision'],state=self.entries[key]['state']))
    def precondition(self,key,tag):
        if tag is None:raise Error(428)
        if not isinstance(tag,str) or not tag.startswith('"cat1:') or not tag.endswith('"') or ',' in tag:raise Error(400)
        if key not in self.entries:raise Error(404)
        if tag!=self.tag(key):raise Error(412)
    def put(self,key,tag,document,resolution=None):
        self.precondition(key,tag);r=self.entries[key]
        if r['state']=='conflict':
            expected=dict(action='rebase' if r['base_hash'] else 'retain-local',expected_imported_record_sha256=r['base_hash'])
            if resolution!=expected:raise Error(409)
        r.update(document=document,state='active',revision=r['revision']+1)
        self.record(key,'update');return self.tag(key)
    def delete(self,key,tag):
        self.precondition(key,tag);r=self.entries[key]
        if r['state']=='conflict':raise Error(409)
        if r['state'] in ('deleted','suppressed'):return self.tag(key)
        r.update(state='suppressed' if r['imported'] else 'deleted',revision=r['revision']+1)
        self.record(key,'delete');return self.tag(key)
    def publish_fixture(self,records):
        """records = complete next selected base. Fixture bypasses staging validation."""
        for key,r in self.entries.items():
            if r['imported'] and key not in records:
                r.update(base_hash=None,imported=False,state='conflict',revision=r['revision']+1);self.record(key,'conflict')
        for key,(signature,doc) in records.items():
            if identity(signature)!=key:raise Error(422)
            h=digest(doc)
            if key not in self.entries:self.entries[key]=dict(signature=signature,document=doc,state='active',revision=0,base_hash=h,imported=True)
            else:
                r=self.entries[key]
                if not r['imported'] or (r['base_hash']!=h and r['revision']>0):
                    r.update(state='conflict',revision=r['revision']+1);self.record(key,'conflict')
                elif r['revision']==0:r['document']=doc
                r.update(base_hash=h,imported=True)
        self.registry_generation+=1
    def cursor(self):return (self.registry_generation,self.overlay_generation)
    def continue_page(self,cursor):
        if cursor!=self.cursor():raise Error(410)

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/dispatch_manifest.py ===
"""Offline generated alias manifest; never executes imported data or enables tools."""
import hashlib,json,sys

def generate(entries):
    seen={}; result=[]
    for entry in sorted(entries,key=lambda e:e['entry_id']):
        canonical=entry['canonical_signature']; bid=hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode()).hexdigest()
        if bid!=entry['entry_id']: raise ValueError('Canonical identity mismatch')
        if bid in seen: raise ValueError('Duplicate effective entry')
        seen[bid]=True
        digest=hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(entry,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'),ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest()
        # Names use the full hash, avoiding collision through human-friendly truncation.
        result.append(dict(tool_name='binding_'+bid,binding_id=bid,schema_hash=digest,
                           generic_binding_id=bid,default_grant='deny',
                           dispatch_state='unavailable',
                           reason='Offline catalog only; runtime authority, target resolver and validated codec required'))
    return dict(format_version='1.0.0',aliases=result)

if __name__=='__main__':
    with open(sys.argv[1]) as f: entries=[json.loads(x) for x in f if x.strip()]
    with open(sys.argv[2],'x') as f:json.dump(generate(entries),f,indent=2)

=== END ARTIFACT ===

"""Metadata-only permission union and concrete AST evidence samples, read-only."""
import pathlib,sqlite3,json,hashlib,sys,collections
out=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve();db=sqlite3.connect('file:'+str(out/'catalog.sqlite')+'?mode=ro',uri=True)
names=[r[0] for r in db.execute('SELECT name FROM permission_definitions UNION SELECT value FROM permission_symbols ORDER BY 1')]
with (out/'permissions.jsonl').open('w') as f:
 for name in names:
  definitions=[dict(source_id=s,protection_level=p,metadata=json.loads(a)) for s,p,a in db.execute('SELECT source_id,protection_level,attributes_json FROM permission_definitions WHERE name=?',(name,))]
  symbols=[dict(symbol=s,source_id=sid,line=n) for s,sid,n in db.execute('SELECT symbol,source_id,line FROM permission_symbols WHERE value=? ORDER BY symbol,source_id,line',(name,))]
  doc=dict(schema_version='1.1.0',permission_id=hashlib.sha256(('permission:'+name).encode()).hexdigest(),name=name,manifest_definitions=definitions,symbols=symbols,metadata_status='manifest-evidence-present' if definitions else 'constant-only; protection-level-and-attributes-unknown',os_authority='unknown',mcp_client_grants='not-inferred')
  f.write(json.dumps(doc,sort_keys=True,separators=(',',':'))+'\n')
