=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/fetch_snapshot.py ===
"""Materialize exact already-selected snapshot blobs; never changes inventory.
Dry-run by default. --download uses the original Google URL at <=1 request/sec,
honors Retry-After on temporary 429, and stops on every other HTTP error.
"""
import pathlib,json,sys,urllib.request,urllib.error,hashlib,base64,time,email.utils,datetime
inv=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]).resolve();root=inv.parent;sources=json.loads(inv.read_text())['sources'];groups={}
for s in sources:
 if s['status']=='downloaded':groups.setdefault(s['git_blob'],s)
pending=[s for s in groups.values() if not (root/s['local_path']).exists()]
print('Missing snapshot blobs:',len(pending),flush=True)
if '--download' not in sys.argv:raise SystemExit('Dry run; pass --download for permitted source retrieval')
for i,s in enumerate(pending):
 for retry in range(3):
  try:
   response=urllib.request.urlopen(s['url']+'?format=TEXT',timeout=45);data=base64.b64decode(response.read())
   assert hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()==s['sha256'];assert hashlib.sha1(b'blob '+str(len(data)).encode()+b'\0'+data).hexdigest()==s['git_blob']
   p=root/s['local_path'];p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);p.write_bytes(data);break
  except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
   if e.code!=429 or retry==2:raise
   header=e.headers.get('Retry-After');pause=60*(retry+1)
   if header:
    try:pause=max(pause,float(header))
    except ValueError:pause=max(pause,(email.utils.parsedate_to_datetime(header)-datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)).total_seconds())
   while pause>0:step=min(pause,60);time.sleep(step);pause-=step
 print(i+1,'/',len(pending),s['path'],flush=True);time.sleep(1)

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/foundation_extract.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline, conservative ingestion foundation. Does not fetch or claim complete coverage.

Input: JSON inventory with sources [{path,url,revision,retrieved_at,sha256,
api_level,channel,kind,license}]. Paths relative to the inventory. Kinds:
metalava, manifest, aidl, evidence. Every source must be hash pinned.
Only use a separately verified, exhaustively enumerated stable source inventory.
"""
import argparse, hashlib, json, pathlib, re, sqlite3

def digest(b): return hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest()

def split_top(text, sep=','):
    result=[]; start=0; stack=[]; quote=None; escape=False
    for i,c in enumerate(text):
        if quote:
            if escape: escape=False
            elif c=='\\': escape=True
            elif c==quote: quote=None
        elif c in '\"\'': quote=c
        elif c in '(<[{': stack.append(c)
        elif c in ')>]}':
            if stack: stack.pop()
        elif c==sep and not stack: result.append(text[start:i].strip());start=i+1
    result.append(text[start:].strip())
    return result

def annotations(text):
    """Balanced annotation scanner, retaining exact expressions and nested annotations."""
    found=[]; clean=[]; pos=0
    while pos<len(text):
        m=re.search(r'@[\w.$]+',text[pos:])
        if not m: clean.append(text[pos:]);break
        a=pos+m.start(); b=pos+m.end();clean.append(text[pos:a]); name=text[a+1:b]
        end=b
        if b<len(text) and text[b]=='(':
            depth=0; quote=None;esc=False
            for end in range(b,len(text)):
                c=text[end]
                if quote:
                    if esc: esc=False
                    elif c=='\\': esc=True
                    elif c==quote: quote=None
                elif c in '\"\'':quote=c
                elif c=='(':depth+=1
                elif c==')':
                    depth-=1
                    if depth==0: end+=1;break
            else: raise ValueError('unclosed annotation')
        found.append((name,text[b+1:end-1] if end>b else '',text[a:end]));pos=end
    return ''.join(clean),found

def permission_ast(name,args):
    short=name.split('.')[-1]
    if short=='RequiresNoPermission':return {'op':'explicit-none'}
    if short=='PermissionManuallyEnforced':return {'op':'manual','reason':'Manual enforcement declared; backend checks unknown'}
    if short in ('Read','Write'):
        _,nested=annotations(args)
        child=permission_ast(nested[0][0],nested[0][1]) if len(nested)==1 else {'op':'unknown','reason':'Unresolved nested annotation','raw':args}
        return {'op':'access','access':short.lower(),'requirement':child}
    pairs={}
    for item in split_top(args):
        if '=' in item:k,v=item.split('=',1);pairs[k.strip()]=v.strip()
        elif item:pairs['value']=item
    def leaf(value):
        if re.fullmatch(r'"[^"\\]+"',value):return {'op':'permission','name':json.loads(value)}
        return {'op':'unknown','reason':'Unresolved symbolic permission expression','raw':value}
    clauses=[]
    for k in ('value','allOf','anyOf'):
        if k not in pairs:continue
        v=pairs[k]
        clauses.append(leaf(v) if k=='value' else {'op':'all' if k=='allOf' else 'any','children':[leaf(x) for x in split_top(v.strip('{}')) if x]})
    ast=clauses[0] if len(clauses)==1 else {'op':'all','children':clauses} if clauses else {'op':'unknown','reason':'Permission expression absent or unsupported','raw':args}
    if pairs.get('conditional')=='true':ast={'op':'conditional','condition':{'op':'unknown','reason':'Annotation marks conditional but does not identify runtime predicate'},'requirement':ast}
    unknown=set(pairs)-{'value','allOf','anyOf','conditional'}
    if unknown:ast={'op':'all','children':[ast,{'op':'unknown','reason':'Unrecognized annotation attributes','raw':args}]}
    return ast

def permission_logic(anns):
    out=[]
    for name,args,raw in anns:
        short=name.split('.')[-1]
        if short not in ('RequiresPermission','Read','Write','EnforcePermission','PermissionManuallyEnforced','RequiresNoPermission'):continue
        if short in ('Read','Write') and 'RequiresPermission' not in name:continue
        if short=='PermissionManuallyEnforced':logic='manual'
        elif short=='RequiresNoPermission':logic='explicit-none'
        elif re.search(r'\bconditional\s*=\s*true',args):logic='conditional'
        elif re.search(r'\ballOf\s*=',args):logic='all'
        elif re.search(r'\banyOf\s*=',args):logic='any'
        else:logic='value'
        pairs={}
        for item in split_top(args):
            if '=' in item:
                k,v=item.split('=',1);pairs[k.strip()]=v.strip()
            elif item:pairs['value']=item
        out.append((name,logic,permission_ast(name,args),raw))
    return out or [(None,'unknown',{'op':'unknown','reason':'No recognized permission annotation in this declaration; absence is not permission-free.'},None)]

def type_name(argument):
    cleaned,_=annotations(argument)
    cleaned=re.sub(r'\b(final|optional)\s+','',cleaned).strip()
    # Metalava v2 normally omits parameter names. Strip only explicit trailing names.
    m=re.match(r'(.+)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)$',cleaned)
    if m and not m.group(1).endswith(('extends','super')):cleaned=m.group(1)
    return re.sub(r'\s+',' ',cleaned)

def parse_signature(line,package,owner):
    clean,anns=annotations(line);clean=clean.strip().rstrip(';')
    kind=clean.split()[0]
    if kind not in ('ctor','method','field','enum_constant','property'):raise ValueError('unsupported signature declaration')
    body=clean[len(kind):].strip()
    if kind in ('ctor','method'):
        a=body.find('(');b=body.rfind(')')
        if a<0 or b<a:raise ValueError('unresolved parameter list')
        head=body[:a].split();name=head[-1];ret=None if kind=='ctor' else head[-2]
        args=[] if not body[a+1:b].strip() else [type_name(x) for x in split_top(body[a+1:b])]
        canonical=owner+'#'+('<init>' if kind=='ctor' else name)+'('+','.join(args)+')'
    else:
        head=body.split('=',1)[0].split();name=head[-1];ret=head[-2];args=[];canonical=owner+'#'+name
    return dict(signature=canonical,package=package,owner=owner,kind=kind,name=name,return_type=ret,arguments_json=json.dumps(args),raw_signature=line),anns

def run(inventory,out):
    inventory=pathlib.Path(inventory);spec=json.loads(inventory.read_text());out=pathlib.Path(out);out.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    dbpath=out/'catalog.sqlite'
    if dbpath.exists():raise ValueError('Refusing to overwrite existing catalog')
    db=sqlite3.connect(dbpath);db.executescript((pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent/'schema.sql').read_text())
    db.execute('INSERT INTO catalog_meta VALUES (?,?)',('coverage','INCOMPLETE: offline ingestion foundation; inspect issues and source inventory'))
    totals={'sources':0,'signature_declarations':0,'parsed_declarations':0,'retained_failures':0}
    def issue(s,n,reason,raw):
        totals['retained_failures']+=1;db.execute('INSERT INTO issues(source_id,line,category,reason,raw) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)',(s,n,'unsupported',reason,raw))
    def binding(row,anns,s,n):
        bid=digest(row['signature'].encode());row={'binding_id':bid,**row}
        db.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO bindings('+','.join(row)+') VALUES ('+','.join('?' for _ in row)+')',list(row.values()))
        db.execute('INSERT INTO occurrences VALUES (?,?,?)',(bid,s,n))
        db.execute('INSERT INTO binding_declarations VALUES (?,?,?,?)',(bid,s,n,json.dumps(row)))
        for name,logic,expression,raw in permission_logic(anns):db.execute('INSERT INTO permission_relations(binding_id,source_id,line,annotation,logic,expression_json,raw_annotation) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(bid,s,n,name,logic,json.dumps(expression),raw))
    for src in spec['sources']:
        path=inventory.parent/src['path'];data=path.read_bytes()
        if digest(data)!=src['sha256']:raise ValueError('Hash mismatch: '+str(path))
        if src['channel']!='stable':raise ValueError('Development snapshots must use a separate catalog')
        if not re.fullmatch('[0-9a-f]{40}',src['revision']) and not src['revision'].startswith('sdk-artifact:'):raise ValueError('Pin immutable commit or exact SDK artifact revision')
        sid=digest((src['url']+'@'+src['revision']+':'+src['sha256']).encode())
        keys=['path','url','revision','retrieved_at','sha256','api_level','channel','kind','license']
        db.execute('INSERT INTO sources VALUES ('+','.join('?' for _ in range(10))+')',[sid]+[src[k] for k in keys]);totals['sources']+=1
        text=data.decode('utf-8')
        if src['kind']=='metalava':
            package=owner=None
            for n,line in enumerate(text.splitlines(),1):
                stripped=line.strip()
                if not stripped or stripped.startswith('//') or stripped=='}':continue
                try:
                    clean,anns=annotations(stripped)
                    if clean.startswith('package '):
                        package=clean.split()[1];row=dict(signature=package,package=package,owner=None,kind='package',name=package,return_type=None,arguments_json='[]',raw_signature=stripped);binding(row,anns,sid,n)
                    elif re.search(r'\b(class|interface|enum|@interface)\s',clean):
                        m=re.search(r'\b(class|interface|enum)\s+([\w.$]+)',clean)
                        if not m:raise ValueError('unsupported class header')
                        owner=package+'.'+m.group(2);row=dict(signature=owner,package=package,owner=owner,kind=m.group(1),name=m.group(2),return_type=None,arguments_json='[]',raw_signature=stripped);binding(row,anns,sid,n)
                    else:
                        totals['signature_declarations']+=1
                        if not owner:raise ValueError('member without owner')
                        row,anns=parse_signature(stripped,package,owner);binding(row,anns,sid,n);totals['parsed_declarations']+=1
                except Exception as e:issue(sid,n,str(e),stripped)
        elif src['kind']=='manifest':
            import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
            ns='{http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android}'
            for p in ET.fromstring(text).iter('permission'):
                db.execute('INSERT INTO permission_definitions VALUES (?,?,?,?)',(p.attrib[ns+'name'],sid,p.attrib.get(ns+'protectionLevel'),json.dumps(p.attrib)))
        elif src['kind']=='aidl':
            code=re.sub(r'/\*.*?\*/|//[^\n]*','',text,flags=re.S)
            p=re.search(r'\bpackage\s+([\w.]+)\s*;',code);d=re.search(r'\b(interface|parcelable|union|enum)\s+(\w+)',code)
            pkg=p.group(1) if p else None;descriptor=pkg+'.'+d.group(2) if p and d and d.group(1)=='interface' else None
            db.execute('INSERT INTO aidl_schemas VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(sid,pkg,descriptor,d.group(1) if d else None,text,'raw-only; members unresolved','{}','unknown'))
            issue(sid,1,'AIDL schema retained intact; member grammar, transactions, imports, permission mappings and codecs are unresolved',text)
        else:issue(sid,None,'Source retained in inventory but unsupported kind',src['kind'])
    db.commit()
    validation={'integrity_check':db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0],'foreign_key_check':db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall(),'counts':{},'reconciliation':totals}
    for table in ('sources','bindings','occurrences','binding_declarations','permission_definitions','permission_relations','aidl_schemas','issues'):
        cursor=db.execute('SELECT * FROM '+table);cols=[x[0] for x in cursor.description];rows=cursor.fetchall();validation['counts'][table]=len(rows)
        with (out/(table+'.jsonl')).open('w') as f:
            for r in rows:f.write(json.dumps(dict(zip(cols,r)),ensure_ascii=False)+'\n')
    assert totals['signature_declarations']==totals['parsed_declarations']+db.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM issues i JOIN sources s USING(source_id) WHERE s.kind='metalava' AND i.raw NOT LIKE '%class %' AND i.raw NOT LIKE '%interface %'").fetchone()[0]
    (out/'validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(validation,indent=2));db.close();return validation

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('inventory');p.add_argument('output');a=p.parse_args();print(json.dumps(run(a.inventory,a.output),indent=2))

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/migrate_v3.py ===
"""Checked offline COPY migration. Never modifies original release or active files."""
import pathlib,sqlite3,json,os,sys
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]

def migrate(source,destination):
    source=pathlib.Path(source).resolve();target=pathlib.Path(destination).resolve()
    staging=target.with_suffix(target.suffix+'.staging')
    if target.exists() or staging.exists():raise ValueError('Destination must be new')
    old=sqlite3.connect(source.as_uri()+'?mode=ro',uri=True);old.row_factory=sqlite3.Row
    if old.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]!='ok' or old.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall():raise ValueError('Source integrity/FK failure')
    new=sqlite3.connect(staging);new.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
    try:
        for p in sorted((P/'migrations').glob('*.sql')):new.executescript(p.read_text())
        tables=[r[0] for r in old.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")]
        new.execute('PRAGMA defer_foreign_keys=ON');new.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
        for table in tables:
            if table=='catalog_view':continue
            for row in old.execute('SELECT * FROM "'+table+'"'):
                r=dict(row)
                if table in ('registries','import_jobs') and 'status' in r:r['state']=r.pop('status')
                if table=='permission_relations':r['logic']=json.loads(r['expression_json'])['op']
                if table=='catalog_meta' and r['key']=='schema_version':r['value']='1.2.0'
                if table=='effective_entries':r['etag']='"cat1:1:'+r['record_sha256']+':'+r['entry_id']+':0:active"'
                cols=','.join('"'+k+'"' for k in r)
                new.execute('INSERT INTO "'+table+'"('+cols+') VALUES('+','.join('?' for _ in r)+')',list(r.values()))
            actual=new.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM "'+table+'"').fetchone()[0]
            expected=old.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM "'+table+'"').fetchone()[0]
            if actual!=expected:raise ValueError('Count mismatch '+table)
        if new.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0]!='ok' or new.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall():raise ValueError('Migrated integrity/FK failure')
        new.commit();new.close();old.close();os.rename(staging,target)
    except Exception:
        new.rollback();new.close();old.close()
        # Preserve failed staging bytes for diagnosis; never present as published output.
        raise

if __name__=='__main__':migrate(sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2])

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/parsers.py ===
"""Conservative source-declaration parsers. No runtime access or wire-codec claims."""
import re,json,hashlib
from foundation_extract import split_top,annotations as old_annotations,permission_ast as old_ast

def sha(x):return hashlib.sha256(x.encode()).hexdigest()
def annotations(text):
 # Equal-length masking preserves source offsets. Quoted @ signs are ordinary data.
 chars=list(text);anns=[];i=0;quote=None;esc=False
 while i<len(text):
  c=text[i]
  if quote:
   if esc:esc=False
   elif c=='\\':esc=True
   elif c==quote:quote=None
   i+=1;continue
  if c in ('"',"'"):quote=c;i+=1;continue
  if c!='@':i+=1;continue
  m=re.match(r'@([\w.$]+)',text[i:])
  if not m:i+=1;continue
  name=m.group(1)
  if name=='interface':i+=len(m.group(0));continue
  a=i;b=i+len(m.group(0));end=b;args='';j=b
  while j<len(text) and text[j].isspace():j+=1
  if j<len(text) and text[j]=='(':
   depth=0;q=None;escape=False;end=None
   for k in range(j,len(text)):
    cc=text[k]
    if q:
     if escape:escape=False
     elif cc=='\\':escape=True
     elif cc==q:q=None
    elif cc in ('"',"'"):q=cc
    elif cc=='(':depth+=1
    elif cc==')':
     depth-=1
     if depth==0:end=k+1;args=text[j+1:k];break
   if end is None:raise ValueError('Unclosed annotation')
  anns.append(dict(name=name,arguments=args,raw=text[a:end],start=a,end=end))
  chars[a:end]=['\n' if x=='\n' else ' ' for x in text[a:end]];i=end
 return ''.join(chars),anns
def unknown(reason,raw=None):
 d=dict(op='unknown',reason=reason)
 if raw is not None:d['raw']=raw
 return d
def permission(anns,symbols=None,aidl=False):
 symbols=symbols or {};clauses=[]
 def resolve(a):
  if a.get('op')=='unknown' and a.get('reason')=='Unresolved symbolic permission expression' and a.get('raw') in symbols:
   return dict(op='permission',name=symbols[a['raw']],**{'x-symbol':a['raw']})
  if a.get('op')=='permission':
   name=a['name']
   if name in symbols:return dict(op='permission',name=symbols[name],**{'x-symbol':name})
   if name.startswith('Manifest.permission.') or '.Manifest.permission.' in name or '.Permission.' in name or re.search(r'\s',name):return unknown('Source annotation contains a symbolic reference or non-permission predicate, not a proven literal permission name',name)
   if aidl and '.' not in name:return unknown('AIDL short permission namespace not resolved by this extractor',name)
  return {k:resolve(v) if isinstance(v,dict) else [resolve(t) if isinstance(t,dict) else t for t in v] if isinstance(v,list) else v for k,v in a.items()}
 for a in anns:
  n=a['name'];short=n.split('.')[-1]
  if short not in ('RequiresPermission','EnforcePermission','PermissionManuallyEnforced','RequiresNoPermission','Read','Write'):continue
  if short in ('Read','Write') and 'RequiresPermission' not in n:continue
  args=a['arguments']
  # AIDL accepts array syntax in addition to Java's brace syntax.
  if aidl:args=re.sub(r'(allOf|anyOf)\s*=\s*\[([^\]]*)\]',r'\1={\2}',args)
  clauses.append(resolve(old_ast(n,args)))
 return clauses[0] if len(clauses)==1 else dict(op='all',children=clauses) if clauses else unknown('No declared recognized permission metadata; absence is not permission-free')

def typ(text):
 clean,_=annotations(text);clean=re.sub(r'\b(final|optional)\s+','',clean).strip()
 clean=re.sub(r'\s+',' ',clean);clean=re.sub(r'\s*([<>,\[\]])\s*',r'\1',clean)
 return clean
def param(text,aidl=False):
 clean,anns=annotations(text);clean=clean.strip();direction=None;name=None
 if aidl:
  m=re.match(r'(inout|in|out)\s+',clean)
  if m:direction=m.group(1);clean=clean[m.end():]
  m=re.fullmatch(r'(.+?)\s+([\w$]+)',clean,re.S)
  if not m:raise ValueError('AIDL parameter type/name unresolved')
  clean,name=m.groups()
 else:
  clean=re.sub(r'\b(final|optional)\s+','',clean)
  m=re.fullmatch(r'(.+?)\s+([A-Za-z_$][\w$]*)(?:\s*=.*)?',clean,re.S)
  if m and not m.group(1).endswith(('extends','super')):clean,name=m.groups()
 return dict(type=typ(clean),name=name,direction=direction,annotations=anns)
def head_return(head):
 h=re.sub(r'^(?:(?:public|protected|private|static|final|abstract|default|synchronized|native|strictfp|deprecated|inline|operator|suspend|oneway)\s+)+','',head).strip()
 if h.startswith('<'):
  depth=0
  for i,c in enumerate(h):
   if c=='<':depth+=1
   elif c=='>':
    depth-=1
    if depth==0:h=h[i+1:].strip();break
 return typ(h)

def member(raw,owner,package,aidl=False):
 clean_full,allanns=annotations(raw);clean=clean_full.strip().rstrip(';').strip()
 kind='aidl-member' if aidl else clean.split()[0]
 body=clean if aidl else clean[len(kind):].strip()
 if '(' in body and (aidl or kind in ('ctor','method')):
  a=body.index('(');b=body.rfind(')')
  if b<a:raise ValueError('Unclosed parameter list')
  h=body[:a].strip();m=re.search(r'([\w$]+)$',h)
  if not m:raise ValueError('Unresolved callable name')
  name=m.group(1);ret=None if kind=='ctor' else head_return(h[:m.start()].strip())
  original_a=clean_full.index('(');original_b=clean_full.rfind(')')
  params=[param(x,aidl) for x in split_top(raw[original_a+1:original_b]) if x.strip()]
  # Capture declaration annotations separately; parameter annotations cannot grant the callable authority.
  anns=[a for a in allanns if a['end']<=original_a]
  canonical=owner+'#'+('<init>' if kind=='ctor' else name)+'('+','.join(p['type'] for p in params)+')'
  extra={}
  if aidl:
   suffix=body[b+1:].strip();explicit=re.fullmatch(r'=\s*(\d+)',suffix)
   extra=dict(oneway=bool(re.search(r'\boneway\b',body[:a])),declared_method_id=int(explicit.group(1)) if explicit else None,wire_transaction_code=None,wire_status='unknown; generated Binder transaction constants and codecs not imported')
   if suffix and not explicit:extra['unresolved_suffix']=suffix
  return dict(kind='constructor' if kind=='ctor' else 'method' if kind=='method' else kind,canonical=canonical,owner=owner,package=package,name=name,return_type=ret,parameters=params,annotations=anns,all_annotations=allanns,raw=raw,extras=extra)
 if aidl:raise ValueError('AIDL non-method statement preserved but not interpreted')
 if kind not in ('field','enum_constant','property'):raise ValueError('Unsupported signature member')
 h=body.split('=',1)[0].strip();m=re.search(r'([\w$]+)$',h)
 if not m:raise ValueError('Unresolved field name')
 name=m.group(1);value=body.split('=',1)[1].strip() if '=' in body else None
 return dict(kind='field' if kind=='enum_constant' else kind,canonical=owner+'#'+name,owner=owner,package=package,name=name,return_type=head_return(h[:m.start()].strip()),parameters=[],annotations=allanns,all_annotations=allanns,raw=raw,extras=dict(constant_raw=value,enum_constant=kind=='enum_constant'))

def signature(text):
 package=None;owner=None
 for line,raw in enumerate(text.splitlines(),1):
  s=raw.strip()
  if not s or s.startswith('//'):continue
  if s=='}':
   if owner is not None:owner=None
   else:package=None
   continue
  try:
   clean,anns=annotations(s)
   if clean.startswith('package '):
    package=clean.split()[1];owner=None;r=dict(kind='package',canonical=package,package=package,owner=None,name=package,parameters=[],return_type=None,annotations=anns,all_annotations=anns,raw=s,extras={})
   else:
    m=re.search(r'(?<![\w.])(@interface|class|interface|enum)\s+([\w.$]+)',clean)
    if m and clean.endswith('{'):
     if not package:raise ValueError('Type without package')
     kind='annotation' if m.group(1)=='@interface' else m.group(1);name=m.group(2);owner=package+'.'+name;r=dict(kind=kind,canonical=owner,package=package,owner=owner,name=name,parameters=[],return_type=None,annotations=anns,all_annotations=anns,raw=s,extras=dict(type_header=clean))
    else:
     if not owner:raise ValueError('Member without owner')
     r=member(s,owner,package)
   r['line']=line;yield r
  except Exception as e:
   if s.endswith('{'):owner=None
   yield dict(kind='unsupported',canonical='unparsed:'+sha(s),package=package or '',owner=owner,name='unparsed',parameters=[],return_type=None,annotations=[],all_annotations=[],raw=s,line=line,extras=dict(parse_error=str(e)))

def without_comments(text):
 # Keep string/char literals intact, including // and /* inside quoted strings.
 return re.sub(r'"(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|\'(?:\\.|[^\'\\])*\'|/\*.*?\*/|//[^\n]*',lambda m:m.group(0) if m.group(0).startswith(('"',"'")) else '\n'*m.group(0).count('\n')+' ',text,flags=re.S)

def aidl(text):
 code=without_comments(text);p=re.search(r'\bpackage\s+([\w.]+)\s*;',code);pkg=p.group(1) if p else ''
 imports=re.findall(r'\bimport\s+([\w.]+)\s*;',code)
 clean,anns=annotations(code)
 d=re.search(r'\b(interface|parcelable|union|enum)\s+([\w]+)',clean)
 if not d:return dict(package=pkg,descriptor=None,declaration_kind=None,imports=imports,annotations=anns,members=[],parse_status='unsupported',reason='No supported top-level AIDL declaration')
 kind,name=d.groups();descriptor=(pkg+'.' if pkg else '')+name
 # Locate the actual declaration before stripping annotations, preserving raw member annotation arguments.
 dm=re.search(r'\b'+kind+r'\s+'+re.escape(name)+r'\b',code)
 prefix=code[:dm.start()];_,topanns=annotations(prefix[prefix.rfind(';')+1:])
 start=code.find('{',dm.end());members=[];status='declaration-only'
 if start>=0:
  depth=0;quote=None;esc=False;end=None
  for i,c in enumerate(code[start:],start):
   if quote:
    if esc:esc=False
    elif c=='\\':esc=True
    elif c==quote:quote=None
   elif c in ('"',"'"):quote=c
   elif c=='{':depth+=1
   elif c=='}':
    depth-=1
    if depth==0:end=i;break
  if end is None:return dict(package=pkg,descriptor=descriptor,declaration_kind=kind,imports=imports,annotations=topanns,members=[],parse_status='unsupported',reason='Unbalanced declaration body')
  body=code[start+1:end]
  if kind=='interface':
   chunks=[c.strip() for c in split_top(body,';') if c.strip()];status='methods-parsed'
   offset=0
   for ordinal,raw in enumerate(chunks):
    locator=body.find(raw,offset);offset=locator+len(raw)
    try:r=member(raw,descriptor,pkg,True);r['extras']['declaration_ordinal']=ordinal
    except Exception as e:r=dict(kind='unsupported',canonical=descriptor+'#unparsed:'+sha(raw),owner=descriptor,package=pkg,name='unparsed',return_type=None,parameters=[],annotations=annotations(raw)[1],all_annotations=annotations(raw)[1],raw=raw,extras=dict(parse_error=str(e)));status='partial'
    r['line']=code[:start+1].count('\n')+1+body[:locator].count('\n');members.append(r)
  else:status='structured-body-retained; fields/enumerants/union tags unresolved'
 return dict(package=pkg,descriptor=descriptor,declaration_kind=kind,imports=imports,annotations=topanns,members=members,parse_status=status,reason='All source bytes retained; generated transaction IDs, resolved imports and wire codecs unknown',oneway=bool(re.search(r'\boneway\s+interface\s+'+re.escape(name),code)))

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/policy_reference.py ===
"""Offline decision algebra. Not authentication, an Android backend or an MCP service."""
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Rule:
    effect: str
    schema_hash: str
    allowed_targets: frozenset
    max_items: int

def evaluate(binding_plan, binding_rules, entry_rule, *, authority, emergency=False):
    """All aliases supply the SAME decoded binding plan; raw unknown uses a raw key.

    Target and numeric bounds here are fixture examples of the closed constraint
    schemas. Real adapters must validate every argument/output field before admission.
    Each tuple is (canonical binding key, exact schema hash, target, requested items).
    """
    if emergency or not binding_plan or entry_rule is None: return 'deny'
    effects=[]
    for key, schema, target, items in binding_plan:
        rule=binding_rules.get(key)
        if rule is None or rule.schema_hash!=schema: return 'deny'
        for r in (rule,entry_rule):
            if r.effect not in ('allow','ask') or target not in r.allowed_targets or not isinstance(items,int) or isinstance(items,bool) or items<0 or items>r.max_items: return 'deny'
            effects.append(r.effect)
    if not authority: return 'os-unavailable'
    return 'ask' if 'ask' in effects else 'admit-candidate'

=== END ARTIFACT ===

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tooling/validate_catalog.py ===
"""Read-only validation of every entry, provenance FK, source reconciliation and AST."""
import pathlib,json,sqlite3,hashlib,collections,sys,datetime
import jsonschema
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
def run(output,inventory):
 out=pathlib.Path(output).resolve();inv=pathlib.Path(inventory).resolve();sources=json.loads(inv.read_text())['sources']
 db=sqlite3.connect('file:'+str(out/'catalog.sqlite')+'?mode=ro',uri=True)
 validator=jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(json.loads((P/'contracts/Entry.schema.json').read_text()))
 ast_validator=jsonschema.Draft202012Validator(json.loads((P/'contracts/PermissionExpression.schema.json').read_text()))
 for f in (P/'contracts').glob('*.schema.json'):jsonschema.Draft202012Validator.check_schema(json.loads(f.read_text()))
 failures=[];kinds=collections.Counter();ops=collections.Counter();variants=0;n=0
 def walk(ast):
  ops[ast['op']]+=1
  for v in ast.values():
   if isinstance(v,dict) and 'op' in v:walk(v)
   elif isinstance(v,list):
    for x in v:
     if isinstance(x,dict) and 'op' in x:walk(x)
 for bid,document,digest,etag in db.execute('SELECT entry_id,document_json,record_sha256,etag FROM effective_entries ORDER BY entry_id'):
  doc=json.loads(document);errors=list(validator.iter_errors(doc))
  if errors:failures.extend(dict(entry_id=bid,path=list(e.path),message=e.message) for e in errors)
  assert hashlib.sha256(doc['canonical_signature'].encode()).hexdigest()==bid
  assert hashlib.sha256(document.encode()).hexdigest()==digest
  assert etag=='"cat1:1:'+digest+':'+bid+':0:active"'
  kinds[doc['kind']]+=1;walk(doc['permission']);variants+='permission_variants' in doc['extensions'];n+=1
  for param in doc['extensions'].get('parameters',[]):
   if 'permission' in param:ast_validator.validate(param['permission'])
  if n%20000==0:print('validated entries',n,flush=True)
 assert n==db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM bindings').fetchone()[0]
 exports=0
 for line in (out/'entries.jsonl').open():
  doc=json.loads(line);exports+=1
  row=db.execute('SELECT record_sha256 FROM effective_entries WHERE entry_id=?',(doc['entry_id'],)).fetchone()
  assert row and hashlib.sha256(line.rstrip('\n').encode()).hexdigest()==row[0]
 assert exports==n
 downloaded=[s for s in sources if s['status']=='downloaded'];assert len(downloaded)==db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM sources').fetchone()[0]
 for s in downloaded:assert hashlib.sha256((inv.parent/s['local_path']).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==s['sha256']
 reports=[json.loads(r[0]) for r in db.execute('SELECT document_json FROM parse_reports')]
 for r in reports:
  if 'expected_declarations' in r:assert r['expected_declarations']==r['declarations']
 signature_stats={}
 for k in ('sdk-signature','source-signature'):
  rs=[r for r in reports if r['kind']==k];signature_stats[k]=dict(files=len(rs),declarations=sum(r['declarations'] for r in rs),unsupported=sum(r['unsupported'] for r in rs))
 integrity=db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0];fk=db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall();assert integrity=='ok' and not fk
 duplicates=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM (SELECT binding_id,source_id,line,count(*) n FROM occurrences GROUP BY binding_id,source_id,line HAVING n>1)').fetchone()[0];assert duplicates==0
 unmatched=db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM annotation_evidence WHERE binding_id IS NULL').fetchone()[0]
 scoped_annotation_ops=collections.Counter()
 for document,scope in db.execute('SELECT document_json,scope FROM annotation_evidence'):
  a=json.loads(document)['annotation']
  if 'permission' in a:ast_validator.validate(a['permission']);scoped_annotation_ops[scope+':'+a['permission']['op']]+=1
 known_defs={x[0] for x in db.execute('SELECT DISTINCT name FROM permission_definitions')};known_symbols={x[0] for x in db.execute('SELECT DISTINCT value FROM permission_symbols')};leaves=collections.Counter()
 for row in db.execute('SELECT expression_json FROM permission_relations'):
  def refs(a):
   if a['op']=='permission':leaves[a['name']]+=1
   for v in a.values():
    if isinstance(v,dict) and 'op' in v:refs(v)
    elif isinstance(v,list):
     for x in v:
      if isinstance(x,dict) and 'op' in x:refs(x)
  refs(json.loads(row[0]))
 result=dict(validated_at=datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).isoformat(),schema_version='1.2.0',entries_validated=n,json_schema_failures=failures,integrity_check=integrity,foreign_key_check=fk,duplicate_occurrences=duplicates,counts={t:db.execute('SELECT count(*) FROM '+t).fetchone()[0] for t in ('sources','bindings','occurrences','binding_declarations','permission_definitions','permission_symbols','permission_relations','aidl_schemas','annotation_evidence','issues')},entry_kinds=dict(kinds),effective_permission_nodes=dict(ops),scoped_annotation_permission_nodes=dict(scoped_annotation_ops),source_dependent_permission_entries=variants,signature_reconciliation=signature_stats,external_annotations_unmatched=unmatched,permission_reference_names=dict(total=len(leaves),with_definition=len(set(leaves)&known_defs),constant_only=len((set(leaves)&known_symbols)-known_defs),without_definition_or_constant=len(set(leaves)-known_defs-known_symbols)),undefined_permission_names=sorted(set(leaves)-known_defs-known_symbols),complete=False)
 db.close();(out/'validation-report.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2));print(json.dumps(result,indent=2),flush=True)
 if failures:raise ValueError('JSON Schema validation failed')
 return result
if __name__=='__main__':run(sys.argv[1],sys.argv[2])

import unittest,pathlib,tempfile,json,hashlib,sqlite3,sys,zipfile

package android {
 public class Manifest.permission {
 field public static final String CAMERA="android.permission.CAMERA";

package a {

 ctor public A();

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_contract_models.py ===
import hashlib,itertools,json,pathlib,sqlite3,sys,unittest
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];sys.path.insert(0,str(P/'tooling'))
from contract_reference import Catalog,Error,identity
from policy_reference import Rule,evaluate
from dispatch_manifest import generate

class Models(unittest.TestCase):
    def error(self,status,fn):
        with self.assertRaises(Error) as e:fn()
        self.assertEqual(e.exception.status,status)
    def test_cas_tombstones_history_and_cursor(self):
        c=Catalog();k=c.create('java:a.A#x()',{'v':1});old=c.tag(k);page=c.cursor()
        self.error(428,lambda:c.put(k,None,{}));self.error(400,lambda:c.put(k,'*',{}))
        deleted=c.delete(k,old);self.error(412,lambda:c.put(k,old,{}))
        self.assertEqual(c.delete(k,deleted),deleted)
        c.put(k,deleted,{'v':2});self.assertEqual(c.entries[k]['state'],'active')
        self.error(410,lambda:c.continue_page(page));self.assertEqual(len(c.history[k]),3)
    def test_import_both_collision_orders_and_rebase(self):
        sig='java:a.A#x()';key=identity(sig);base={key:(sig,{'p':1})}
        c=Catalog();c.create(sig,{});c.publish_fixture(base)
        self.assertEqual(c.entries[key]['state'],'conflict');self.error(409,lambda:c.delete(key,c.tag(key)))
        self.error(409,lambda:c.put(key,c.tag(key),{}));c.put(key,c.tag(key),{},dict(action='rebase',expected_imported_record_sha256=c.entries[key]['base_hash']))
        d=Catalog();d.publish_fixture(base);self.error(409,lambda:d.create(sig,{}))
        d.delete(key,d.tag(key));d.publish_fixture({key:(sig,{'p':2})});self.assertEqual(d.entries[key]['state'],'conflict')
        d.publish_fixture({});d.put(key,d.tag(key),{},dict(action='retain-local',expected_imported_record_sha256=None));self.assertFalse(d.entries[key]['imported'])
    def test_binding_rules_dominate_all_aliases(self):
        plan=[('binding','s','t',2)]
        for effect,entry_effect,authority,emergency in itertools.product(('deny','ask','allow'),('deny','ask','allow'),(False,True),(False,True)):
            rule=Rule(effect,'s',frozenset({'t'}),3);entry=Rule(entry_effect,'tool',frozenset({'t'}),3)
            decisions=[evaluate(plan,{'binding':rule},entry,authority=authority,emergency=emergency) for route in ('generated','generic','known-raw')]
            self.assertEqual(len(set(decisions)),1)
            if effect=='deny' or entry_effect=='deny' or emergency:self.assertEqual(decisions[0],'deny')
        allow=Rule('allow','s',frozenset({'t'}),1)
        self.assertEqual(evaluate(plan,{'binding':allow},allow,authority=True),'deny')
        self.assertEqual(evaluate(plan,{},allow,authority=True),'deny')
        self.assertEqual(evaluate([('raw:service','s','t',1)],{'binding':allow},allow,authority=True),'deny')
    def test_generated_alias_identity_and_default_deny(self):
        sig='java:a.A#x()';e=dict(entry_id=identity(sig),canonical_signature=sig)
        r=generate([e])['aliases'][0];self.assertEqual(r['binding_id'],r['generic_binding_id']);self.assertEqual(r['default_grant'],'deny')
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):generate([e,e])
    def test_bridge_ddl_integrity_and_closed_enums(self):
        db=sqlite3.connect(':memory:');db.executescript((P/'bridge-config.sql').read_text())
        db.execute("INSERT INTO clients VALUES('fixture','fixture','active',0,1,0,0,NULL)")
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("INSERT INTO credentials VALUES('fixture','missing',zeroblob(32),'fixture','active',0,NULL,NULL,0)")
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):db.execute("INSERT INTO client_logging_policy VALUES('fixture','full-raw',1,1,1,1)")
        self.assertEqual(db.execute('PRAGMA integrity_check').fetchone()[0],'ok');self.assertEqual(db.execute('PRAGMA foreign_key_check').fetchall(),[])

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_parsers.py ===
import unittest,pathlib,sys,json
sys.path.insert(0,str(pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'tooling'))
import parsers as p
class ParserTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_overloads_and_annotations(self):
  text='''package a {
 public @interface Ann {
 method public abstract String value();
 }
 public class Outer.Inner<T> {
 ctor public Outer.Inner();
 method @RequiresPermission("p") public <T extends java.lang.Object> java.util.List<T> go(@Nullable java.util.Map<String,java.util.List<T>>, int);
 method public void go(int);
 field public static final String S = "hello // no comment";
 }
 }'''
  rs=list(p.signature(text));self.assertFalse(any(r['kind']=='unsupported' for r in rs));self.assertEqual(rs[1]['kind'],'annotation');ms=[x for x in rs if x['name']=='go'];self.assertNotEqual(ms[0]['canonical'],ms[1]['canonical']);self.assertEqual(ms[0]['parameters'][0]['type'],'java.util.Map<String,java.util.List<T>>');self.assertEqual(ms[0]['return_type'],'java.util.List<T>');self.assertEqual(p.permission(ms[0]['annotations'])['name'],'p')
 def test_permission_modes(self):
  for text,op in [('@RequiresPermission(value="x")','permission'),('@RequiresPermission(allOf={"a","b"})','all'),('@RequiresPermission(anyOf={"a","b"},conditional=true)','conditional'),('@RequiresPermission.Read(@RequiresPermission("x"))','access'),('@PermissionManuallyEnforced','manual'),('@RequiresNoPermission','explicit-none'),('','unknown')]:self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations(text)[1])['op'],op)
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@RequiresPermission(android.Manifest.permission.X)')[1],{'android.Manifest.permission.X':'android.permission.X'})['name'],'android.permission.X')
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@EnforcePermission("X")')[1],aidl=True)['op'],'unknown')
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@RequiresPermission("carrier privileges")')[1])['op'],'unknown')
  self.assertEqual(p.permission(p.annotations('@RequiresPermission.Read')[1])['op'],'access')
 def test_parameter_permission_not_callable(self):
  r=p.member('method public void x(@RequiresPermission("p") String);','a.A','a');self.assertEqual(p.permission(r['annotations'])['op'],'unknown');self.assertTrue(r['parameters'][0]['annotations'])
 def test_aidl(self):
  text='''package a; import b.B;
 @EnforcePermission("android.permission.X") oneway interface IFoo {
 @RequiresNoPermission void ping(in B b, out int[] value) = 7;
 @PermissionManuallyEnforced oneway void send(in String s);
 const int C=3;
 }'''
  d=p.aidl(text);self.assertEqual(d['descriptor'],'a.IFoo');self.assertTrue(d['oneway']);self.assertEqual(len(d['members']),3);self.assertEqual(d['members'][0]['extras']['declared_method_id'],7);self.assertIsNone(d['members'][0]['extras']['wire_transaction_code']);self.assertEqual(d['members'][0]['parameters'][1]['direction'],'out');self.assertEqual(d['members'][2]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertEqual(p.permission(d['annotations'],aidl=True)['name'],'android.permission.X')
 def test_source_unknown_retained(self):
  r=list(p.signature('package a {\n strange new declaration;\n}'));self.assertEqual(r[1]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertEqual(r[1]['raw'],'strange new declaration;')
 def test_owner_reset_after_type(self):
  rs=list(p.signature('package a {\n public class A {\n method public void x();\n }\n method public void orphan();\n}'))
  self.assertEqual(rs[-1]['kind'],'unsupported');self.assertIn('without owner',rs[-1]['extras']['parse_error'])
 def test_comment_quoted(self):self.assertIn('"//x"',p.without_comments('const String X="//x"; // hi'))
 def test_aidl_structured(self):
  d=p.aidl('package a; @JavaDerive(toString=true) parcelable P { int n; }');self.assertEqual(d['descriptor'],'a.P');self.assertIn('retained',d['parse_status'])
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

=== UNTRUSTED ARTIFACT catalog/tests/test_remediation.py ===
"""Regression evidence for recovered Claude findings; offline fixtures only."""
import json, pathlib, sqlite3, sys, tempfile, unittest
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
sys.path.insert(0,str(P/'tooling'))
import parsers

def database():
    db=sqlite3.connect(':memory:')
    for p in sorted((P/'migrations').glob('*.sql')): db.executescript(p.read_text())
    return db

class ContractRegressions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_natural_primary_keys_are_not_null(self):
        db=database()
        violations=[]
        for (table,) in db.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'"):
            for _,name,typ,nn,_,pk in db.execute('PRAGMA table_info('+table+')'):
                if pk and typ!='INTEGER' and not nn: violations.append(table+'.'+name)
        self.assertEqual(violations,[])

    def test_registry_state_round_trip(self):
        db=database()
        states=['staged','validated','rejected','published','retired']
        for n,state in enumerate(states):
            db.execute('INSERT INTO registries(registry_id,registry_version,schema_version,android_version,api_level,source_set_hash,created_at,state,validation_json) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',(str(n),n,'1.2.0','fixture',37,'x','now',state,'{}'))
        self.assertEqual([x[0] for x in db.execute('SELECT state FROM registries ORDER BY registry_version')],states)

    def test_permission_root_vocabulary(self):
        db=database()
        ddl=db.execute("SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name='permission_relations'").fetchone()[0]
        self.assertNotIn("'value'",ddl)
        self.assertIn("'access'",ddl)
        self.assertIn("'permission'",ddl)

    def test_offline_copy_migration(self):
        from migrate_v3 import migrate
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
            r=pathlib.Path(td);old=sqlite3.connect(r/'old.sqlite')
            for name in ('001-initial.sql','002-evidence-and-publication.sql'):
                old.executescript((P.parent/'evidence'/('original-'+name)).read_text())
            old.execute("INSERT INTO registries VALUES('fixture',1,'1.1.0','fixture',37,'hash','now','published','{}')");old.commit();old.close()
            before=(r/'old.sqlite').read_bytes();migrate(r/'old.sqlite',r/'new.sqlite')
            self.assertEqual(before,(r/'old.sqlite').read_bytes())
            new=sqlite3.connect(r/'new.sqlite');self.assertEqual(new.execute('SELECT state FROM registries').fetchone()[0],'published')
            self.assertEqual(new.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0],3)

class ParserRegressions(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_symbolic_read_and_header_do_not_leak_owner(self):
        rs=list(parsers.signature('package a {\n public @interface Ann {\n method @RequiresPermission.Read(@RequiresPermission(android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA)) public String x();\n }\n method public void orphan();\n}'))
        self.assertEqual(rs[1]['kind'],'annotation')
        ast=parsers.permission(rs[2]['annotations'],{'android.Manifest.permission.CAMERA':'android.permission.CAMERA'})
        self.assertEqual(ast['requirement']['name'],'android.permission.CAMERA')
        self.assertEqual(rs[-1]['kind'],'unsupported')

    def test_repeated_aidl_statement_locators_are_distinct(self):
        d=parsers.aidl('package a; interface I {\n void x();\n void x();\n}')
        self.assertNotEqual(d['members'][0]['line'],d['members'][1]['line'])

if __name__=='__main__': unittest.main()
